        m_engine.setPaused(false);
        m_engine.tick(1.0f / 60.0f);
        m_engine.setPaused(true);
        m_stepOnce = false;
    } else {
        m_engine.tick(deltaTime);
    }
    if (m_lookState.animateStress) m_engine.setAnimation(static_cast<float>(now), m_movingObjectCount, 1);

    if (io.MouseDelta.x != 0.0f || io.MouseDelta.y != 0.0f || io.MouseWheel != 0.0f || ImGui::IsAnyMouseDown() || io.InputQueueCharacters.Size > 0 || ImGui::IsKeyDown(ImGuiKey_Space) || io.KeyCtrl || io.KeyShift || io.KeyAlt) m_lastInputTime = now;
    for (int key = ImGuiKey_NamedKey_BEGIN; key < ImGuiKey_NamedKey_END && m_lastInputTime != now; ++key) {
        if (ImGui::IsKeyDown(static_cast<ImGuiKey>(key))) m_lastInputTime = now;
    }
    if (!ctx.playing && m_autosaveInterval > 0.0f) {
        m_autosaveTimer += deltaTime;
        if (m_autosaveTimer >= m_autosaveInterval && now - m_lastInputTime >= 3.0) {
            m_autosaveTimer = 0.0f;
            if (ctx.dirty() && ctx.world.saveScene(kAutosavePath)) ctx.toast("Autosaved");
        }
    }
    if (const size_t over = m_engine.renderOverCapacity(); over != m_reportedOverCapacity) {
        m_reportedOverCapacity = over;
        if (over > 0) ctx.toast(std::format("{} entities exceed GPU memory; the viewport is paused. Undo (Ctrl+Z) or delete entities to resume.", over), ToastKind::Error);
    }
    trackRenderSettings();
    updateTitle();
    m_engine.update();
    InputManager::Update();
    m_engine.present();
    if (m_framesPresented++ == 0) Lit::Log::Phase("first frame presented");
    glfwPollEvents();
}

```

Editor/Autopilot.cppm
```
module;

#include <cstdint>
#include <functional>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

export module Editor.autopilot;

import Editor.context;
import Editor.commands;

export class Autopilot {
  public:
    bool load(const std::string& path);
    [[nodiscard]] bool active() const { return m_active; }
    void beforeFrame(EditorContext& ctx, CommandRegistry& commands, float dt);
    [[nodiscard]] int failures() const { return m_failures; }
    [[nodiscard]] bool finished() const { return m_finished; }
    std::function<std::string(const std::string&)> query;
    std::function<void(const std::string&)> saveScene;
    std::function<void(const std::string&)> loadScene;
    std::function<bool(const std::string&, float)> editMaterial;
    void* window = nullptr;

  private:
    struct Step {
        std::string verb;
        std::vector<std::string> args;
        int line = 0;
    };

    bool m_active = false;
    bool m_finished = false;
    std::vector<Step> m_steps;
    size_t m_next = 0;
    float m_wait = 0.0f;
    int m_waitFrames = 0;
    int m_failures = 0;
    int m_checks = 0;
    std::vector<int> m_releaseKeys;
    bool m_releaseMouse[3] = {false, false, false};
    int m_holdFrames = 0;
    bool m_mouseSet = false;
    float m_mouse[2] = {0.0f, 0.0f};
    int m_measureFrames = 0;
    float m_measureBudget = 0.0f;
    std::string m_measureLabel;
    std::vector<float> m_measureSamples;
};

Editor/Commands.cppm
```
module;

#include <imgui.h>

#include <cstdint>
#include <functional>
#include <string>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <vector>

export module Editor.commands;

export struct Chord {
    ImGuiKey key = ImGuiKey_None;
    bool ctrl = false;
    bool shift = false;
    bool alt = false;
};

export Chord key(ImGuiKey k) { return {k, false, false, false}; }
export Chord ctrl(ImGuiKey k) { return {k, true, false, false}; }
export Chord shift(ImGuiKey k) { return {k, false, true, false}; }
export Chord alt(ImGuiKey k) { return {k, false, false, true}; }
export Chord ctrlShift(ImGuiKey k) { return {k, true, true, false}; }
export Chord ctrlAlt(ImGuiKey k) { return {k, true, false, true}; }

export enum class CommandScope { Global, Viewport };

export struct Command {
    std::string id;
    std::string title;
    std::string category;
    std::vector<Chord> chords;
    std::function<void()> run;
    std::function<bool()> enabled;
    std::function<bool()> checked;
    CommandScope scope = CommandScope::Viewport;
    bool repeat = false;
    bool palette = true;
    bool help = true;
    std::string keysLabel;
    std::string description;
};

export std::string chordLabel(const Chord& chord);

export class CommandRegistry {
  public:
    Command& add(Command command);
    [[nodiscard]] const Command* find(const std::string& id) const;
    bool run(const std::string& id);
    void processShortcuts(bool viewportKeys);
    [[nodiscard]] const std::vector<Command>& all() const { return m_commands; }
    [[nodiscard]] std::string shortcut(const std::string& id) const;
    [[nodiscard]] uint32_t uses(const std::string& id) const;
    [[nodiscard]] uint64_t lastUse(const std::string& id) const;

  private:
    std::vector<Command> m_commands;
    std::unordered_map<std::string, size_t> m_index;
    std::unordered_map<std::string, uint32_t> m_uses;
    std::unordered_map<std::string, uint64_t> m_lastUse;
    uint64_t m_clock = 0;
};

Editor/Autopilot/Autopilot.cpp
```
module;

#include <GLFW/glfw3.h>
#include <imgui.h>
#include <imgui_impl_glfw.h>

#include <algorithm>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <format>
#include <fstream>
#include <sstream>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>

#include "Engine/Log/Log.hpp"

module Editor.autopilot;

import Editor.context;
import Editor.commands;
import Engine.engine;
import Engine.World;
import Engine.History;
import Engine.Render.settings;
import Engine.Render.custom;

namespace {

ImGuiKey keyByName(const std::string& name) {
    for (int k = ImGuiKey_NamedKey_BEGIN; k < ImGuiKey_NamedKey_END; ++k) {
        const char* keyName = ImGui::GetKeyName(static_cast<ImGuiKey>(k));
        if (keyName && name == keyName) return static_cast<ImGuiKey>(k);
    }
    if (name == "[") return ImGuiKey_LeftBracket;
    if (name == "]") return ImGuiKey_RightBracket;
    if (name == "`") return ImGuiKey_GraveAccent;
    if (name == "=") return ImGuiKey_Equal;
    if (name == "/") return ImGuiKey_Slash;
    if (name == "Esc") return ImGuiKey_Escape;
    if (name == "Del") return ImGuiKey_Delete;
    return ImGuiKey_None;
}

int mouseButton(const std::string& name) {
    if (name == "right") return ImGuiMouseButton_Right;
    if (name == "middle") return ImGuiMouseButton_Middle;
    return ImGuiMouseButton_Left;
}

}  // namespace

bool Autopilot::load(const std::string& path) {
    std::ifstream in(path);
    if (!in) {
        Lit::Log::Error("Autopilot script '{}' not found", path);
        return false;
    }
    std::string line;
    int number = 0;
    while (std::getline(in, line)) {
        ++number;
        const size_t hash = line.find('#');
        if (hash != std::string::npos && line.rfind("text", 0) != 0) line = line.substr(0, hash);
        std::istringstream words(line);
        Step step;
        step.line = number;
        if (!(words >> step.verb)) continue;
        if (step.verb == "text" || step.verb == "log") {
            std::string rest;
            std::getline(words, rest);
            if (!rest.empty() && rest.front() == ' ') rest.erase(0, 1);
            step.args.push_back(rest);
        } else {
            std::string arg;
            while (words >> arg) step.args.push_back(arg);
        }
        m_steps.push_back(std::move(step));
    }
    m_active = true;
    Lit::Log::Info("Autopilot loaded {} steps from {}", m_steps.size(), path);
    return true;
}

void Autopilot::beforeFrame(EditorContext& ctx, CommandRegistry& commands, float dt) {
    if (!m_active) return;
    ImGuiIO& io = ImGui::GetIO();
    auto pinMouse = [&] {
        if (!m_mouseSet) return;
        if (GLFWwindow* w = static_cast<GLFWwindow*>(window)) {
            ImGui_ImplGlfw_CursorEnterCallback(w, GLFW_TRUE);
            ImGui_ImplGlfw_CursorPosCallback(w, m_mouse[0], m_mouse[1]);
        } else {
            io.AddMousePosEvent(m_mouse[0], m_mouse[1]);
        }
    };
    auto setMouse = [&](float x, float y) {
        m_mouseSet = true;
        m_mouse[0] = x;
        m_mouse[1] = y;
        pinMouse();
    };
    pinMouse();
    if (m_measureFrames > 0) {
        m_measureSamples.push_back(dt * 1000.0f);
        if (--m_measureFrames > 0) return;
        std::vector<float> sorted = m_measureSamples;
        std::sort(sorted.begin(), sorted.end());
        const float median = sorted[sorted.size() / 2];
        const float p95 = sorted[std::min(sorted.size() - 1, sorted.size() * 95 / 100)];
        Lit::Log::Info("[autopilot] measure {}: {} frames, median {:.2f} ms ({:.1f} fps), p95 {:.2f} ms, max {:.2f} ms", m_measureLabel, sorted.size(), median, 1000.0f / median, p95, sorted.back());
        if (m_measureBudget > 0.0f) {
            ++m_checks;
            if (median > m_measureBudget) {
                Lit::Log::Error("[autopilot] FAIL {}: median {:.2f} ms exceeds budget {:.2f} ms", m_measureLabel, median, m_measureBudget);
                ++m_failures;
            }
        }
        m_measureSamples.clear();
    }
    if (m_holdFrames > 0) {
        --m_holdFrames;
        return;
    }
    for (int key : m_releaseKeys) io.AddKeyEvent(static_cast<ImGuiKey>(key), false);
    m_releaseKeys.clear();
    for (int b = 0; b < 3; ++b) {
        if (m_releaseMouse[b]) io.AddMouseButtonEvent(b, false);
        m_releaseMouse[b] = false;
    }
    io.AddKeyEvent(ImGuiMod_Ctrl, false);
    io.AddKeyEvent(ImGuiMod_Shift, false);
    io.AddKeyEvent(ImGuiMod_Alt, false);

    if (m_waitFrames > 0) {
        --m_waitFrames;
        return;
    }
    if (m_wait > 0.0f) {
        m_wait -= dt;
        return;
    }
    while (m_next < m_steps.size()) {
        const Step& step = m_steps[m_next++];
        const auto& a = step.args;
        auto arg = [&](size_t i, const std::string& fallback = {}) { return i < a.size() ? a[i] : fallback; };
        auto num = [&](size_t i, float fallback = 0.0f) { return i < a.size() ? static_cast<float>(std::atof(a[i].c_str())) : fallback; };
        auto coord = [&](size_t i, bool vertical) {
            const float value = num(i);
            if (i < a.size() && !a[i].empty() && a[i].back() == '%') return value * 0.01f * (vertical ? io.DisplaySize.y : io.DisplaySize.x);
            return value;
        };
        if (step.verb == "wait") {
            m_wait = num(0, 0.1f);
            return;
        }
        if (step.verb == "measure") {
            m_measureFrames = std::max(1, static_cast<int>(num(0, 120.0f)));
            m_measureLabel = arg(1, "frames");
            m_measureBudget = num(2, 0.0f);
            m_measureSamples.clear();
            return;
        }
        if (step.verb == "frames") {
            m_waitFrames = static_cast<int>(num(0, 1.0f));
            return;
        }
        if (step.verb == "key" || step.verb == "down") {
            const ImGuiKey key = keyByName(arg(0));
            if (key == ImGuiKey_None) {
                Lit::Log::Error("Autopilot line {}: unknown key '{}'", step.line, arg(0));
                ++m_failures;
                continue;
            }
            for (size_t i = 1; i < a.size(); ++i) {
                if (a[i] == "ctrl") io.AddKeyEvent(ImGuiMod_Ctrl, true);
                if (a[i] == "shift") io.AddKeyEvent(ImGuiMod_Shift, true);
                if (a[i] == "alt") io.AddKeyEvent(ImGuiMod_Alt, true);
            }
            io.AddKeyEvent(key, true);
            if (step.verb == "key") {
                m_releaseKeys.push_back(key);
                m_holdFrames = 1;
            }
            return;
        }
        if (step.verb == "up") {
            io.AddKeyEvent(keyByName(arg(0)), false);
            return;
        }
        if (step.verb == "text") {
            io.AddInputCharactersUTF8(arg(0).c_str());
            return;
        }
        if (step.verb == "move") {
            setMouse(coord(0, false), coord(1, true));
            return;
        }
        if (step.verb == "click") {
            const int button = mouseButton(arg(0, "left"));
            if (a.size() >= 3) setMouse(coord(1, false), coord(2, true));
            io.AddMouseButtonEvent(button, true);
            m_releaseMouse[button] = true;
            m_holdFrames = 1;
            return;
        }
        if (step.verb == "press") {
            for (size_t i = 1; i < a.size(); ++i) {
                if (a[i] == "ctrl") io.AddKeyEvent(ImGuiMod_Ctrl, true);
                if (a[i] == "shift") io.AddKeyEvent(ImGuiMod_Shift, true);
                if (a[i] == "alt") io.AddKeyEvent(ImGuiMod_Alt, true);
            }
            io.AddMouseButtonEvent(mouseButton(arg(0, "left")), true);
            return;
        }
        if (step.verb == "release") {
            io.AddMouseButtonEvent(mouseButton(arg(0, "left")), false);
            return;
        }
        if (step.verb == "wheel") {
            io.AddMouseWheelEvent(0.0f, num(0, 1.0f));
            return;
        }
        if (step.verb == "run") {
            if (!commands.run(arg(0))) {
                Lit::Log::Error("Autopilot line {}: command '{}' did not run", step.line, arg(0));
                ++m_failures;
            }
            return;
        }
        if (step.verb == "set") {
            static const std::pair<std::string_view, std::string_view> aliases[] = {{"time", "timeOfDay"}, {"ev", "manualEV100"}, {"compensation", "exposureCompensation"}, {"debug", "debugView"}, {"cascades", "shadowCascades"}};
            std::string key = arg(0);
            for (const auto& [from, to] : aliases) {
                if (key == from) key = to;
            }
            std::string value;
            for (size_t i = 1; i < a.size(); ++i) value += (i > 1 ? " " : "") + a[i];
            if (!setRenderSetting(ctx.engine.renderSettings(), key, value)) {
                Lit::Log::Error("Autopilot line {}: unknown setting '{}'", step.line, key);
                ++m_failures;
            }
            continue;
        }
        if (step.verb == "pass") {
            bool found = false;
            std::string name;
            for (size_t i = 0; i + 1 < a.size(); ++i) name += (i > 0 ? " " : "") + a[i];
            for (const PassInfo& pass : ctx.engine.customPasses()) {
                if (pass.name != name) continue;
                ctx.engine.setPassEnabled(pass.kind, pass.id, num(a.size() - 1) > 0.5f);
                found = true;
            }
            if (!found) {
                Lit::Log::Error("Autopilot line {}: no pass named '{}'", step.line, name);
                ++m_failures;
            }
            continue;
        }
        if (step.verb == "material") {
            if (!editMaterial || !editMaterial(arg(0), num(1))) {
                Lit::Log::Error("Autopilot line {}: cannot set material field '{}'", step.line, arg(0));
                ++m_failures;
            }
            continue;
        }
        if (step.verb == "save" || step.verb == "load") {
            const auto& action = step.verb == "save" ? saveScene : loadScene;
            if (action) action(arg(0, "resources/autopilot.litscene"));
            m_waitFrames = 2;
            return;
        }
        if (step.verb == "shot") {
            ctx.engine.requestScreenshot(arg(0, "resources/autopilot.png"));
            m_waitFrames = 2;
            return;
        }
        if (step.verb == "log") {
            Lit::Log::Info("[autopilot] {}", arg(0));
            continue;
        }
        if (step.verb == "expect") {
            ++m_checks;
            const std::string what = arg(0);
            std::string actual;
            if (what == "selection") actual = std::to_string(ctx.selection.size());
            if (what == "entities") actual = std::to_string(ctx.world.aliveCount());
            if (what == "undo") actual = std::to_string(ctx.history->undoCount());
            if (what == "mode") actual = ctx.modeLabel;
            if (what == "playing") actual = ctx.playing ? "1" : "0";
            if (what == "name") actual = ctx.world.isAlive(ctx.primary()) ? ctx.world.getName(ctx.primary()) : "";
            if (what.rfind("setting:", 0) == 0) actual = renderSettingValue(ctx.engine.renderSettings(), what.substr(8));
            if (actual.empty() && query) actual = query(what);
            std::string expected = arg(1);
            for (size_t i = 2; i < a.size(); ++i) expected += " " + a[i];
            if (actual == expected) {
                Lit::Log::Info("[autopilot] PASS {} == {}", what, expected);
            } else {
                Lit::Log::Error("[autopilot] FAIL line {}: {} is '{}', expected '{}'", step.line, what, actual, expected);
                ++m_failures;
            }
            continue;
        }
        if (step.verb == "quit") {
            m_finished = true;
            m_active = false;
            Lit::Log::Info("[autopilot] finished: {} checks, {} failures", m_checks, m_failures);
            ctx.requestQuit = true;
            return;
        }
        Lit::Log::Error("Autopilot line {}: unknown verb '{}'", step.line, step.verb);
        ++m_failures;
    }
    m_finished = true;
    m_active = false;
    Lit::Log::Info("[autopilot] finished: {} checks, {} failures", m_checks, m_failures);
}

```

Editor/Hierarchy/Hierarchy.cppm
```
module;

#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <vector>

export module Editor.hierarchy;

import Editor.context;
import Engine.Render.entity;

export class Outliner {
  public:
    void draw(EditorContext& ctx);
    void focusSearch() { m_focusSearch = true; }
    void reveal(EditorContext& ctx, EntityHandle e);

  private:
    struct Row {
        EntityHandle entity;
        uint16_t depth = 0;
        bool hasChildren = false;
    };

    struct Query {
        std::string text;
        std::string tag;
        std::string mesh;
        int layer = -1;
        int visible = -1;
        bool selectedOnly = false;
        bool active = false;
    };

    void rebuild(EditorContext& ctx);
    void runSearch(EditorContext& ctx);
    [[nodiscard]] bool matches(EditorContext& ctx, EntityHandle e) const;
    void drawRow(EditorContext& ctx, const Row& row, int index);
    void parseQuery();

    std::vector<Row> m_rows;
    size_t m_totalRoots = 0;
    bool m_truncated = false;
    uint64_t m_builtStructure = 0;
    uint64_t m_builtExpansion = 1;
    uint64_t m_expansionVersion = 0;
    double m_lastBuild = -1.0;
    std::unordered_set<uint32_t> m_expanded;
    std::string m_search;
    std::string m_lastSearch;
    Query m_query;
    Entity m_searchCursor = 0;
    std::vector<EntityHandle> m_searchResults;
    bool m_searchDone = true;
    bool m_focusSearch = false;
    EntityHandle m_renaming;
    std::string m_renameBuffer;
    bool m_renameFocus = false;
    EntityHandle m_scrollTo;
    EntityHandle m_rangeAnchor;
};

```

Editor/Hierarchy/Hierarchy.cpp
```
module;

#include <imgui.h>
#include <imgui_internal.h>
#include <imgui_stdlib.h>

#include <algorithm>
#include <cctype>
#include <cstdint>
#include <format>
#include <sstream>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

module Editor.hierarchy;

import Editor.context;
import Engine.engine;
import Engine.World;
import Engine.History;
import Engine.Render.entity;
import Engine.Render.component;
import Engine.glm;

namespace {

constexpr size_t kMaxRows = 200000;
constexpr size_t kMaxResults = 5000;
constexpr size_t kSearchBudget = 300000;

std::string lower(std::string s) {
    for (char& c : s) c = static_cast<char>(std::tolower(static_cast<unsigned char>(c)));
    return s;
}

void drawEye(ImDrawList* draw, ImVec2 center, float r, bool visible, ImU32 color) {
    draw->PathArcTo(ImVec2(center.x, center.y + r * 0.9f), r * 1.35f, -2.35f, -0.79f, 12);
    draw->PathArcTo(ImVec2(center.x, center.y - r * 0.9f), r * 1.35f, 0.79f, 2.35f, 12);
    draw->PathStroke(color, ImDrawFlags_Closed, 1.3f);
    if (visible) {
        draw->AddCircleFilled(center, r * 0.42f, color, 12);
    } else {
        draw->AddLine(ImVec2(center.x - r, center.y + r * 0.8f), ImVec2(center.x + r, center.y - r * 0.8f), color, 1.4f);
    }
}

}  // namespace

void Outliner::parseQuery() {
    m_query = Query{};
    std::istringstream in(m_search);
    std::string token;
    std::string text;
    while (in >> token) {
        const std::string t = lower(token);
        if (t.rfind("tag:", 0) == 0) {
            m_query.tag = token.substr(4);
        } else if (t.rfind("mesh:", 0) == 0) {
            m_query.mesh = lower(token.substr(5));
        } else if (t.rfind("layer:", 0) == 0) {
            m_query.layer = std::atoi(token.c_str() + 6);
        } else if (t == "vis:0" || t == "hidden") {
            m_query.visible = 0;
        } else if (t == "vis:1" || t == "visible") {
            m_query.visible = 1;
        } else if (t == "sel" || t == "selected") {
            m_query.selectedOnly = true;
        } else {
            if (!text.empty()) text += ' ';
            text += t;
        }
    }
    m_query.text = text;
    m_query.active = !m_search.empty();
}

bool Outliner::matches(EditorContext& ctx, EntityHandle e) const {
    World& world = ctx.world;
    if (m_query.selectedOnly && !ctx.selection.contains(e)) return false;
    if (m_query.visible >= 0 && world.isVisible(e) != (m_query.visible == 1)) return false;
    if (m_query.layer >= 0 && (world.getLayer(e) & (1u << m_query.layer)) == 0) return false;
    if (!m_query.tag.empty() && world.getTag(e) != m_query.tag) return false;
    if (!m_query.mesh.empty() && lower(meshLabel(ctx.engine, world.getMesh(e))).find(m_query.mesh) == std::string::npos) return false;
    if (!m_query.text.empty()) {
        const std::string name = lower(displayName(world, e));
        if (name.find(m_query.text) == std::string::npos) return false;
    }
    return true;
}

void Outliner::runSearch(EditorContext& ctx) {
    if (m_searchDone) return;
    World& world = ctx.world;
    const size_t slots = world.database().transforms.size();
    size_t budget = kSearchBudget;
    while (m_searchCursor < slots && budget-- > 0 && m_searchResults.size() < kMaxResults) {
        const EntityHandle e = world.handleOf(m_searchCursor++);
        if (!e.isNull() && matches(ctx, e)) m_searchResults.push_back(e);
    }
    if (m_searchCursor >= slots || m_searchResults.size() >= kMaxResults) m_searchDone = true;
}

void Outliner::rebuild(EditorContext& ctx) {
    World& world = ctx.world;
    m_rows.clear();
    m_truncated = false;
    struct Frame {
        EntityHandle next;
        uint16_t depth;
    };
    std::vector<Frame> frames{{world.firstRoot(), 0}};
    while (!frames.empty()) {
        if (m_rows.size() >= kMaxRows) {
            m_truncated = true;
            break;
        }
        Frame& frame = frames.back();
        if (frame.next.isNull()) {
            frames.pop_back();
            continue;
        }
        const EntityHandle current = frame.next;
        const uint16_t depth = frame.depth;
        frame.next = world.nextSibling(current);
        const EntityHandle child = world.firstChild(current);
        m_rows.push_back({current, depth, !child.isNull()});
        if (!child.isNull() && m_expanded.count(current.index)) frames.push_back({child, static_cast<uint16_t>(depth + 1)});
    }
    m_builtStructure = world.database().m_structureVersion;
    m_builtExpansion = m_expansionVersion;
    m_lastBuild = ImGui::GetTime();
}

void Outliner::reveal(EditorContext& ctx, EntityHandle e) {
    if (!ctx.world.isAlive(e)) return;
    for (EntityHandle p = ctx.world.getParent(e); !p.isNull(); p = ctx.world.getParent(p)) m_expanded.insert(p.index);
    ++m_expansionVersion;
    m_scrollTo = e;
}

void Outliner::drawRow(EditorContext& ctx, const Row& row, int index) {
    World& world = ctx.world;
    const EntityHandle e = row.entity;
    if (!world.isAlive(e)) {
        ImGui::TextDisabled("(destroyed)");
        return;
    }
    ImGui::PushID(static_cast<int>(e.index));
    ImGui::PushID(static_cast<int>(e.generation));
    const float rowHeight = ImGui::GetFrameHeight();
    const ImVec2 start = ImGui::GetCursorScreenPos();
    const float width = ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail().x;
    const float indent = static_cast<float>(row.depth) * 14.0f;
    const bool selected = ctx.selection.contains(e);
    const bool primary = ctx.primary() == e;
    const bool visible = world.isVisible(e);
    ImDrawList* draw = ImGui::GetWindowDrawList();

    ImGui::SetNextItemAllowOverlap();
    if (ImGui::Selectable("##row", selected, ImGuiSelectableFlags_AllowDoubleClick | ImGuiSelectableFlags_SpanAvailWidth, ImVec2(0.0f, rowHeight))) {
        const ImGuiIO& io = ImGui::GetIO();
        if (ImGui::IsMouseDoubleClicked(ImGuiMouseButton_Left)) {
            m_renaming = e;
            m_renameBuffer = world.getName(e);
            m_renameFocus = true;
        } else if (io.KeyCtrl) {
            ctx.selection.toggle(e);
        } else if (io.KeyShift && world.isAlive(m_rangeAnchor)) {
            int anchor = -1;
            for (int i = 0; i < static_cast<int>(m_rows.size()); ++i) {
                if (m_rows[i].entity == m_rangeAnchor) anchor = i;
            }
            if (anchor >= 0 && !m_query.active) {
                for (int i = std::min(anchor, index); i <= std::max(anchor, index); ++i) ctx.selection.add(m_rows[i].entity);
                ctx.selection.remove(e);
                ctx.selection.add(e);
            } else {
                ctx.selection.add(e);
            }
        } else {
            ctx.selection.set(e);
            m_rangeAnchor = e;
        }
    }
    if (ImGui::IsItemHovered()) ctx.hovered = e;

    if (ImGui::BeginDragDropSource()) {
        if (!ctx.selection.contains(e)) ctx.selection.set(e);
        ImGui::SetDragDropPayload("LIT_ENTITY", &e, sizeof(EntityHandle));
        ImGui::Text("Move %zu under...", ctx.selection.size());
        ImGui::EndDragDropSource();
    }
    if (ImGui::BeginDragDropTarget()) {
        if (ImGui::AcceptDragDropPayload("LIT_ENTITY")) {
            ctx.history->beginGroup("Reparent");
            for (EntityHandle moved : ctx.topLevelSelection()) {
                if (moved != e && !world.isDescendantOf(e, moved)) ctx.history->setParent(moved, e);
            }
            ctx.history->endGroup();
            m_expanded.insert(e.index);
            ++m_expansionVersion;
        }
        ImGui::EndDragDropTarget();
    }

    if (ImGui::BeginPopupContextItem("row menu")) {
        if (!ctx.selection.contains(e)) ctx.selection.set(e);
        if (ImGui::MenuItem("Rename", "F2")) {
            m_renaming = e;
            m_renameBuffer = world.getName(e);
            m_renameFocus = true;
        }
        if (ImGui::MenuItem("Focus", "F")) {
            const glm::vec4 b = world.getWorldBounds(e);
            flyTo(ctx, b.w > 0.0f ? b : glm::vec4(world.getWorldPosition(e), 1.0f));
        }
        if (ImGui::MenuItem(visible ? "Hide" : "Show", "H")) ctx.history->setVisible(e, !visible);
        if (ImGui::MenuItem("Select children")) {
            for (EntityHandle c : world.getChildren(e)) ctx.selection.add(c);
        }
        if (ImGui::MenuItem("Unparent", "Alt+P", false, !world.getParent(e).isNull())) ctx.history->setParent(e, NULL_ENTITY);
        ImGui::Separator();
        if (ImGui::MenuItem("Delete", "Del")) {
            ctx.history->beginGroup("Delete");
            for (EntityHandle d : ctx.topLevelSelection()) ctx.history->destroy(d);
            ctx.history->endGroup();
            ctx.selection.clear();
        }
        ImGui::EndPopup();
    }

    const float arrowX = start.x + indent + 4.0f;
    const float centerY = start.y + rowHeight * 0.5f;
    if (row.hasChildren && !m_query.active) {
        const bool open = m_expanded.count(e.index) > 0;
        ImGui::SetCursorScreenPos(ImVec2(arrowX - 2.0f, start.y));
        if (ImGui::InvisibleButton("##arrow", ImVec2(14.0f, rowHeight))) {
            if (open) {
                m_expanded.erase(e.index);
            } else {
                m_expanded.insert(e.index);
            }
            ++m_expansionVersion;
        }
        ImGui::RenderArrow(draw, ImVec2(arrowX, centerY - ImGui::GetFontSize() * 0.36f), IM_COL32(170, 176, 188, 255), open ? ImGuiDir_Down : ImGuiDir_Right, 0.7f);
    }

    const float textX = arrowX + 16.0f;
    const ImU32 textColor = !visible ? IM_COL32(120, 124, 132, 255) : primary ? IM_COL32(255, 200, 110, 255) : IM_COL32(230, 232, 236, 255);
    if (m_renaming == e) {
        ImGui::SetCursorScreenPos(ImVec2(textX, start.y));
        ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(width - (textX - start.x) - 30.0f);
        if (m_renameFocus) {
            ImGui::SetKeyboardFocusHere();
            m_renameFocus = false;
        }
        const bool done = ImGui::InputText("##rename", &m_renameBuffer, ImGuiInputTextFlags_EnterReturnsTrue | ImGuiInputTextFlags_AutoSelectAll);
        if (done || ImGui::IsItemDeactivated()) {
            if (!ImGui::IsKeyPressed(ImGuiKey_Escape)) ctx.history->setName(e, m_renameBuffer);
            m_renaming = NULL_ENTITY;
        }
    } else {
        const std::string name = displayName(world, e);
        draw->AddText(ImVec2(textX, centerY - ImGui::GetFontSize() * 0.5f), textColor, name.c_str());
        const uint32_t mesh = world.getMesh(e);
        const std::string meta = mesh == NO_MESH ? std::string("empty") : meshLabel(ctx.engine, mesh);
        const ImVec2 metaSize = ImGui::CalcTextSize(meta.c_str());
        const float metaX = start.x + width - metaSize.x - 30.0f;
        if (metaX > textX + ImGui::CalcTextSize(name.c_str()).x + 12.0f) draw->AddText(ImVec2(metaX, centerY - ImGui::GetFontSize() * 0.5f), IM_COL32(110, 116, 128, 255), meta.c_str());
    }

    ImGui::SetCursorScreenPos(ImVec2(start.x + width - 22.0f, start.y));
    if (ImGui::InvisibleButton("##eye", ImVec2(20.0f, rowHeight))) ctx.history->setVisible(e, !visible);
    const bool eyeHover = ImGui::IsItemHovered();
    drawEye(draw, ImVec2(start.x + width - 12.0f, centerY), 5.0f, visible, eyeHover ? IM_COL32(255, 190, 90, 255) : visible ? IM_COL32(170, 176, 188, 200) : IM_COL32(110, 114, 122, 200));
    ImGui::SetCursorScreenPos(ImVec2(start.x, start.y + rowHeight + ImGui::GetStyle().ItemSpacing.y * 0.25f));
    ImGui::Dummy(ImVec2(0.0f, 0.0f));
    ImGui::PopID();
    ImGui::PopID();
}

void Outliner::draw(EditorContext& ctx) {
    if (!ctx.showOutliner || ctx.zen) return;
    const ImGuiViewport* viewport = ImGui::GetMainViewport();
    ImGui::SetNextWindowPos(ImVec2(viewport->WorkPos.x + 10.0f, viewport->WorkPos.y + 10.0f), ImGuiCond_FirstUseEver);
    ImGui::SetNextWindowSize(ImVec2(300.0f, viewport->WorkSize.y * 0.62f), ImGuiCond_FirstUseEver);
    ImGui::SetNextWindowBgAlpha(0.90f);
    ctx.outlinerFocused = false;
    if (!ImGui::Begin("Scene", &ctx.showOutliner, ImGuiWindowFlags_NoCollapse | ImGuiWindowFlags_NoFocusOnAppearing)) {
        ImGui::End();
        return;
    }
    ctx.outlinerFocused = ImGui::IsWindowFocused(ImGuiFocusedFlags_RootAndChildWindows);
    ctx.panels.emplace_back(ImGui::GetWindowPos().x, ImGui::GetWindowPos().y, ImGui::GetWindowSize().x, ImGui::GetWindowSize().y);

    if (m_focusSearch) {
        ImGui::SetKeyboardFocusHere();
        m_focusSearch = false;
    }
    ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(-1.0f);
    if (ImGui::InputTextWithHint("##search", "Filter   name  tag:x  mesh:cube  layer:2  hidden  sel", &m_search)) {
        parseQuery();
        m_searchResults.clear();
        m_searchCursor = 0;
        m_searchDone = !m_query.active;
    }
    if (ImGui::IsItemActive() && ImGui::IsKeyPressed(ImGuiKey_Escape)) {
        m_search.clear();
        parseQuery();
        m_searchDone = true;
    }

    World& world = ctx.world;
    const bool stale = m_builtStructure != world.database().m_structureVersion || m_builtExpansion != m_expansionVersion;
    if (stale && (m_builtExpansion != m_expansionVersion || ImGui::GetTime() - m_lastBuild > 0.2)) rebuild(ctx);
    if (m_query.active) runSearch(ctx);

    const size_t total = world.aliveCount();
    if (m_query.active) {
        ImGui::TextDisabled("%zu match%s%s", m_searchResults.size(), m_searchResults.size() == 1 ? "" : "es", m_searchDone ? "" : "  (scanning...)");
    } else {
        ImGui::TextDisabled("%zu entities%s", total, m_truncated ? "  (first 200k rows, use filter)" : "");
    }
    ImGui::SameLine();
    const float buttonWidth = ImGui::CalcTextSize("Collapse").x + 12.0f;
    ImGui::SetCursorPosX(ImGui::GetWindowContentRegionMax().x - buttonWidth);
    if (ImGui::SmallButton("Collapse")) {
        m_expanded.clear();
        ++m_expansionVersion;
    }
    ImGui::Separator();

    ImGui::BeginChild("rows", ImVec2(0.0f, 0.0f), ImGuiChildFlags_None);
    const int count = static_cast<int>(m_query.active ? m_searchResults.size() : m_rows.size());
    const float rowStride = ImGui::GetFrameHeight() + ImGui::GetStyle().ItemSpacing.y * 0.25f;
    if (!m_scrollTo.isNull() && !m_query.active) {
        for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
            if (m_rows[i].entity == m_scrollTo) {
                const float y = static_cast<float>(i) * rowStride;
                if (y < ImGui::GetScrollY() || y > ImGui::GetScrollY() + ImGui::GetWindowHeight() - rowStride) ImGui::SetScrollY(y - ImGui::GetWindowHeight() * 0.4f);
                m_scrollTo = NULL_ENTITY;
                break;
            }
        }
        if (m_builtExpansion == m_expansionVersion) m_scrollTo = NULL_ENTITY;
    }
    ImGuiListClipper clipper;
    clipper.Begin(count, rowStride);
    while (clipper.Step()) {
        for (int i = clipper.DisplayStart; i < clipper.DisplayEnd; ++i) {
            if (m_query.active) {
                const EntityHandle e = m_searchResults[i];
                drawRow(ctx, Row{e, 0, false}, i);
            } else {
                drawRow(ctx, m_rows[i], i);
            }
        }
    }
    clipper.End();

    if (ImGui::IsWindowFocused() && !ImGui::GetIO().WantTextInput && !m_query.active && count > 0) {
        int current = -1;
        for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
            if (m_rows[i].entity == ctx.primary()) {
                current = i;
                break;
            }
        }
        int next = current;
        if (ImGui::IsKeyPressed(ImGuiKey_DownArrow)) next = std::min(current + 1, count - 1);
        if (ImGui::IsKeyPressed(ImGuiKey_UpArrow)) next = std::max(current - 1, 0);
        if (current >= 0 && ImGui::IsKeyPressed(ImGuiKey_RightArrow) && m_rows[current].hasChildren) {
            m_expanded.insert(m_rows[current].entity.index);
            ++m_expansionVersion;
        }
        if (current >= 0 && ImGui::IsKeyPressed(ImGuiKey_LeftArrow)) {
            if (m_expanded.erase(m_rows[current].entity.index)) {
                ++m_expansionVersion;
            } else if (!world.getParent(m_rows[current].entity).isNull()) {
                ctx.selection.set(world.getParent(m_rows[current].entity));
                m_scrollTo = ctx.primary();
            }
        }
        if (next != current && next >= 0) {
            if (ImGui::GetIO().KeyShift) {
                ctx.selection.add(m_rows[next].entity);
            } else {
                ctx.selection.set(m_rows[next].entity);
            }
            m_scrollTo = m_rows[next].entity;
            m_rangeAnchor = m_rows[next].entity;
        }
        if (current >= 0 && (ImGui::IsKeyPressed(ImGuiKey_F2) || ImGui::IsKeyPressed(ImGuiKey_Enter))) {
            m_renaming = m_rows[current].entity;
            m_renameBuffer = world.getName(m_renaming);
            m_renameFocus = true;
        }
    }

    ImGui::Dummy(ImVec2(ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail().x, std::max(ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail().y, 24.0f)));
    if (ImGui::BeginDragDropTarget()) {
        if (ImGui::AcceptDragDropPayload("LIT_ENTITY")) {
            ctx.history->beginGroup("Unparent");
            for (EntityHandle moved : ctx.topLevelSelection()) ctx.history->setParent(moved, NULL_ENTITY);
            ctx.history->endGroup();
        }
        ImGui::EndDragDropTarget();
    }
    if (ImGui::IsItemClicked() && !ImGui::GetIO().KeyCtrl && !ImGui::GetIO().KeyShift) ctx.selection.clear();
    ImGui::EndChild();
    ImGui::End();
}


```

Editor/Context.cppm
```
module;

#include <array>
#include <cstdint>
#include <functional>
#include <memory>
#include <optional>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

#include "Engine/Log/Log.hpp"

export module Editor.context;

import Engine.engine;
import Engine.World;
import Engine.History;
import Engine.Selection;
import Engine.Render.entity;
import Engine.camera;
import Engine.glm;

export enum class ToastKind { Info, Success, Warning, Error };

export struct Toast {
    std::string text;
    ToastKind kind = ToastKind::Info;
    float age = 0.0f;
};

export struct CameraBookmark {
    glm::vec3 position{0.0f};
    float yaw = 0.0f;
    float pitch = 0.0f;
    bool valid = false;
};

export enum class HandleMode { Translate, Rotate, Scale, Off };

export struct CameraFlight {
    bool active = false;
    glm::vec3 from{0.0f};
    glm::vec3 to{0.0f};
    float fromYaw = 0.0f, toYaw = 0.0f, fromPitch = 0.0f, toPitch = 0.0f;
    float t = 0.0f;
    float duration = 0.25f;
    float fromOrtho = 0.0f;
    float toOrtho = 0.0f;
};

export struct EditorContext {
    EditorContext(Engine& engine) : engine(engine), world(engine.world()), history(std::make_unique<History>(engine.world())) {}

    Engine& engine;
    World& world;
    std::unique_ptr<History> history;
    Selection selection;

    HandleMode handles = HandleMode::Translate;
    bool localSpace = false;
    bool snapping = false;
    float snapMove = 0.5f;
    float snapAngle = 15.0f;
    float snapScale = 0.1f;

    bool playing = false;
    bool paused = false;
    WorldSnapshot playSnapshot;

    bool showOutliner = true;
    bool showInspector = true;
    bool pinInspector = false;
    bool showWorld = false;
    bool worldDismissed = false;
    bool showLook = false;
    bool showStats = true;
    bool showHelp = false;
    bool showTimeline = false;
    bool zen = false;
    bool showGrid = true;
    bool openPalette = false;
    std::string paletteSeed;
    bool openQuickAdd = false;
    bool requestQuit = false;

    std::array<std::vector<EntityHandle>, 10> selectionSets{};
    std::array<CameraBookmark, 10> bookmarks{};
    std::vector<Prefab> clipboard;

    std::string scenePath = "resources/scene.litscene";
    uint64_t savedRevision = 0;

    std::vector<Toast> toasts;

    EntityHandle hovered;
    glm::vec3 cursor{0.0f};
    bool cursorOnSurface = false;
    bool cursorValid = false;
    glm::vec2 mouse{0.0f};
    bool viewportHovered = true;
    bool outlinerFocused = false;
    bool modalActive = false;
    bool flying = false;
    float flySpeed = 8.0f;
    bool autoOrtho = false;
    CameraFlight flight;

    std::string modeLabel = "EDIT";
    std::vector<std::pair<std::string, std::string>> hints;
    std::vector<glm::vec4> panels;
    float rightColumnTop = 122.0f;

    uint32_t cubeMesh = 0;
    uint32_t sphereMesh = 0;
    EntityHandle stressParent;

    void toast(std::string text, ToastKind kind = ToastKind::Info) {
        if (toasts.size() >= 6) toasts.erase(toasts.begin());
        if (kind == ToastKind::Error) {
            Lit::Log::Error("[toast] {}", text);
        } else {
            Lit::Log::Info("[toast] {}", text);
        }
        toasts.push_back({std::move(text), kind, 0.0f});
    }

    void hint(std::string keys, std::string action) { hints.emplace_back(std::move(keys), std::move(action)); }

    [[nodiscard]] bool dirty() const { return history->state() != savedRevision; }

    [[nodiscard]] EntityHandle primary() const { return selection.primary(); }

    void select(EntityHandle e) {
        if (e.isNull()) {
            selection.clear();
        } else {
            selection.set(e);
        }
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<EntityHandle> topLevelSelection() const {
        std::vector<EntityHandle> out;
        for (EntityHandle e : selection.items()) {
            if (!world.isAlive(e)) continue;
            bool nested = false;
            for (EntityHandle p = world.getParent(e); !p.isNull(); p = world.getParent(p)) {
                if (selection.contains(p)) {
                    nested = true;
                    break;
                }
            }
            if (!nested) out.push_back(e);
        }
        return out;
    }
};

export std::string displayName(World& world, EntityHandle e);
export std::string meshLabel(Engine& engine, uint32_t mesh);
export void applyEditorTheme(float scale);
export struct SpawnPoint {
    glm::vec3 position{0.0f};
    bool onSurface = false;
};
export SpawnPoint spawnPoint(EditorContext& ctx);
export std::optional<glm::vec3> surfacePoint(EditorContext& ctx, const Ray& ray, const RayHit& hit);
export void flyTo(EditorContext& ctx, const glm::vec4& sphere);
export void updateFlight(EditorContext& ctx, float dt);
export glm::vec3 cameraForward(const Camera& camera);
export std::optional<double> evaluateExpression(const std::string& text);
export float fuzzyScore(const std::string& pattern, const std::string& text);

```

Editor/Look/Look.cpp
```
module;

#include <imgui.h>
#include <imgui_stdlib.h>

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdint>
#include <format>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

module Editor.look;

import Editor.context;
import Engine.engine;
import Engine.renderer;
import Engine.World;
import Engine.camera;
import Engine.Render.settings;
import Engine.Render.custom;
import Engine.glm;

namespace {

bool sunDial(const char* id, float& hours, float& azimuth) {
    ImGui::PushID(id);
    const float size = std::min(ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail().x, 150.0f);
    const ImVec2 origin = ImGui::GetCursorScreenPos();
    ImGui::InvisibleButton("dial", ImVec2(size, size * 0.62f));
    const bool active = ImGui::IsItemActive();
    ImDrawList* draw = ImGui::GetWindowDrawList();
    const ImVec2 center(origin.x + size * 0.5f, origin.y + size * 0.55f);
    const float radius = size * 0.45f;
    draw->AddRectFilledMultiColor(ImVec2(origin.x, origin.y), ImVec2(origin.x + size, center.y), IM_COL32(40, 70, 130, 255), IM_COL32(40, 70, 130, 255), IM_COL32(230, 150, 80, 255), IM_COL32(230, 150, 80, 255));
    draw->AddRectFilled(ImVec2(origin.x, center.y), ImVec2(origin.x + size, origin.y + size * 0.62f), IM_COL32(35, 32, 30, 255));
    draw->PathArcTo(center, radius, 3.14159f, 6.28318f, 32);
    draw->PathStroke(IM_COL32(255, 255, 255, 60), 0, 1.5f);
    const float t = std::clamp((hours - 6.0f) / 12.0f, -0.25f, 1.25f);
    const float angle = 3.14159f + t * 3.14159f;
    const ImVec2 sun(center.x + std::cos(angle) * radius, center.y + std::sin(angle) * radius);
    const bool night = hours < 6.0f || hours > 18.0f;
    draw->AddCircleFilled(sun, 7.0f, night ? IM_COL32(180, 190, 220, 255) : IM_COL32(255, 220, 120, 255), 16);
    draw->AddText(ImVec2(origin.x + 6.0f, origin.y + 4.0f), IM_COL32(255, 255, 255, 220), std::format("{:02d}:{:02d}", static_cast<int>(hours) % 24, static_cast<int>(std::fmod(hours, 1.0f) * 60.0f)).c_str());
    bool changed = false;
    if (active) {
        const ImVec2 mouse = ImGui::GetIO().MousePos;
        const float a = std::atan2(mouse.y - center.y, mouse.x - center.x);
        float tt = (a < 0.0f ? a + 6.28318f : a) / 3.14159f - 1.0f;
        if (mouse.y > center.y) tt = mouse.x < center.x ? -0.1f : 1.1f;
        hours = std::clamp(6.0f + tt * 12.0f, 0.0f, 24.0f);
        if (ImGui::GetIO().KeyShift) azimuth = std::fmod(azimuth + ImGui::GetIO().MouseDelta.x + 360.0f, 360.0f);
        changed = true;
    }
    if (ImGui::IsItemHovered()) ImGui::SetTooltip("Drag the sun across the sky. Shift+drag spins its azimuth.");
    ImGui::PopID();
    return changed;
}

}  // namespace

void LookPanel::draw(EditorContext& ctx, LookState& state) {
    const double gpu = ctx.engine.gpuTotalMilliseconds();
    m_gpuHistory[m_historyHead] = static_cast<float>(gpu);
    m_historyHead = (m_historyHead + 1) % m_gpuHistory.size();
    if (!ctx.showLook || ctx.zen) return;
    const ImGuiViewport* viewport = ImGui::GetMainViewport();
    const float top = ctx.rightColumnTop;
    ImGui::SetNextWindowPos(ImVec2(viewport->WorkPos.x + viewport->WorkSize.x - 10.0f, top), ImGuiCond_Always, ImVec2(1.0f, 0.0f));
    ImGui::SetNextWindowSize(ImVec2(380.0f, std::max(240.0f, viewport->WorkPos.y + viewport->WorkSize.y - top - 44.0f)), ImGuiCond_Always);
    ImGui::SetNextWindowBgAlpha(0.93f);
    if (!ImGui::Begin("Look###LookPanel", &ctx.showLook, ImGuiWindowFlags_NoCollapse | ImGuiWindowFlags_NoFocusOnAppearing | ImGuiWindowFlags_NoMove | ImGuiWindowFlags_NoResize)) {
        ImGui::End();
        return;
    }
    ctx.panels.emplace_back(ImGui::GetWindowPos().x, ImGui::GetWindowPos().y, ImGui::GetWindowSize().x, ImGui::GetWindowSize().y);
    Engine& engine = ctx.engine;
    RenderSettings& r = engine.renderSettings();
    Camera& camera = ctx.world.camera();
    ImGui::PushItemWidth(-120.0f);

    if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Sky and sun", ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_DefaultOpen)) {
        int sky = static_cast<int>(r.sky);
        if (ImGui::Combo("Sky", &sky, "Physical atmosphere\0Gradient\0HDRI\0")) r.sky = static_cast<SkyMode>(sky);
        sunDial("sun", r.timeOfDay, r.sunAzimuth);
        ImGui::SliderFloat("Time of day", &r.timeOfDay, 0.0f, 24.0f, "%.2f h");
        ImGui::SliderFloat("Sun azimuth", &r.sunAzimuth, 0.0f, 360.0f, "%.0f°");
        ImGui::Checkbox("Animate time", &r.animateTime);
        if (r.animateTime) {
            ImGui::SameLine();
            ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(-1.0f);
            ImGui::SliderFloat("##speed", &r.timeSpeed, 0.01f, 4.0f, "%.2f h/s", ImGuiSliderFlags_Logarithmic);
        }
        ImGui::Checkbox("Sun follows a Sun light entity", &r.sunFollowsLight);
        ImGui::DragFloat("Sun illuminance", &r.sunIlluminance, 500.0f, 0.0f, 200000.0f, "%.0f lux");
        ImGui::SliderFloat("Sun size", &r.sunAngularDiameter, 0.1f, 5.0f, "%.2f°");
        if (r.sky == SkyMode::Physical && ImGui::TreeNode("Atmosphere")) {
            ImGui::SliderFloat("Rayleigh", &r.rayleighScale, 0.0f, 4.0f);
            ImGui::SliderFloat("Mie (haze)", &r.mieScale, 0.0f, 10.0f, "%.2f", ImGuiSliderFlags_Logarithmic);
            ImGui::SliderFloat("Mie anisotropy", &r.mieAnisotropy, 0.0f, 0.95f);
            ImGui::SliderFloat("Ozone", &r.ozoneScale, 0.0f, 3.0f);
            ImGui::SliderFloat("Ground albedo", &r.groundAlbedo, 0.0f, 1.0f);
            ImGui::SliderFloat("Aerial distance", &r.aerialDistanceScale, 0.0f, 20.0f);
            ImGui::TreePop();
        }
        if (r.sky == SkyMode::Physical && ImGui::TreeNode("Clouds and stars")) {
            ImGui::Checkbox("Clouds", &r.clouds);
            ImGui::SliderFloat("Coverage", &r.cloudCoverage, 0.0f, 1.0f);
            ImGui::SliderFloat("Density", &r.cloudDensity, 0.0f, 3.0f);
            ImGui::SliderFloat("Wind", &r.cloudSpeed, 0.0f, 80.0f, "%.1f m/s");
            ImGui::DragFloat("Altitude", &r.cloudAltitude, 10.0f, 200.0f, 12000.0f, "%.0f m");
            ImGui::Checkbox("Stars at night", &r.stars);
            ImGui::TreePop();
        }
        if (r.sky == SkyMode::Gradient) {
            ImGui::ColorEdit3("Zenith", &r.gradientZenith.x);
            ImGui::ColorEdit3("Horizon", &r.gradientHorizon.x);
            ImGui::ColorEdit3("Ground", &r.gradientGround.x);
            ImGui::DragFloat("Brightness", &r.gradientIntensity, 50.0f, 0.0f, 100000.0f, "%.0f nits");
        }
        if (r.sky == SkyMode::Hdri) {
            ImGui::InputTextWithHint("HDRI file", "resources/sky.hdr", &r.hdriPath);
            ImGui::DragFloat("Brightness", &r.hdriIntensity, 50.0f, 0.0f, 200000.0f, "%.0f nits");
            ImGui::SliderFloat("Rotation", &r.hdriRotation, 0.0f, 360.0f, "%.0f°");
        }
        ImGui::SliderFloat("Environment light", &r.environmentIntensity, 0.0f, 4.0f);
        ImGui::SliderFloat("Sky brightness", &r.skyBrightness, 0.0f, 4.0f);
    }
    if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Camera and exposure", ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_DefaultOpen)) {
        ImGui::Checkbox("Auto exposure", &r.autoExposure);
        ImGui::SameLine();
        ImGui::TextDisabled("EV100 %.1f", engine.exposureEv100());
        if (r.autoExposure) {
            ImGui::SliderFloat("Compensation", &r.exposureCompensation, -5.0f, 5.0f, "%+.1f EV");
            ImGui::SliderFloat("Adapt brighter", &r.adaptationUp, 0.1f, 10.0f);
            ImGui::SliderFloat("Adapt darker", &r.adaptationDown, 0.1f, 10.0f);
            ImGui::DragFloatRange2("EV range", &r.minEV100, &r.maxEV100, 0.1f, -10.0f, 20.0f, "%.1f", "%.1f");
        } else {
            ImGui::SliderFloat("EV100", &r.manualEV100, -6.0f, 18.0f, "%.1f");
            if (ImGui::Button("Sunny 16")) r.manualEV100 = 15.0f;
            ImGui::SameLine();
            if (ImGui::Button("Overcast")) r.manualEV100 = 12.0f;
            ImGui::SameLine();
            if (ImGui::Button("Indoor")) r.manualEV100 = 7.0f;
            ImGui::SameLine();
            if (ImGui::Button("Night")) r.manualEV100 = 0.0f;
        }
        float fov = camera.getFov();
        if (ImGui::SliderFloat("Field of view", &fov, 10.0f, 120.0f, "%.0f°")) camera.setFov(fov);
        float nearPlane = camera.getNearPlane();
        float farPlane = camera.getFarPlane();
        if (ImGui::DragFloat("Near", &nearPlane, 0.005f, 0.001f, 10.0f, "%.3f")) camera.setNearPlane(std::max(nearPlane, 0.001f));
        if (ImGui::DragFloat("Far", &farPlane, 5.0f, 10.0f, 1000000.0f, "%.0f")) camera.setFarPlane(std::max(farPlane, nearPlane + 1.0f));
        ImGui::SliderFloat("Fly speed", &ctx.flySpeed, 0.1f, 500.0f, "%.1f", ImGuiSliderFlags_Logarithmic);
    }
    if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Color grading", ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_DefaultOpen)) {
        int op = static_cast<int>(r.tonemapper);
        if (ImGui::Combo("Tonemapper", &op, "AgX\0AgX punchy\0ACES\0Khronos PBR Neutral\0Reinhard\0Linear\0")) r.tonemapper = static_cast<Tonemapper>(op);
        ImGui::SliderFloat("Contrast", &r.contrast, 0.5f, 1.5f);
        ImGui::SliderFloat("Saturation", &r.saturation, 0.0f, 2.0f);
        ImGui::SliderFloat("White balance", &r.temperature, 2000.0f, 12000.0f, "%.0f K");
        ImGui::SliderFloat("Tint", &r.tint, -1.0f, 1.0f);
        ImGui::SliderFloat("Vignette", &r.vignette, 0.0f, 1.0f);
        ImGui::SliderFloat("Film grain", &r.grain, 0.0f, 1.0f);
        ImGui::Checkbox("Bloom", &r.bloom);
        if (r.bloom) {
            ImGui::SliderFloat("Bloom strength", &r.bloomIntensity, 0.0f, 0.3f, "%.3f");
            ImGui::SliderFloat("Bloom radius", &r.bloomRadius, 0.1f, 1.0f);
        }
        ImGui::Checkbox("FXAA", &r.fxaa);
    }
    if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Shadows")) {
        ImGui::Checkbox("Cascaded shadows", &r.shadows);
        ImGui::SliderInt("Cascades", &r.shadowCascades, 1, 4);
        int resolutionIndex = r.shadowResolution >= 4096 ? 3 : r.shadowResolution >= 2048 ? 2 : r.shadowResolution >= 1024 ? 1 : 0;
        if (ImGui::Combo("Resolution", &resolutionIndex, "512\0" "1024\0" "2048\0" "4096\0")) r.shadowResolution = 512 << resolutionIndex;
        ImGui::DragFloat("Distance", &r.shadowDistance, 1.0f, 10.0f, 2000.0f, "%.0f m");
        ImGui::Checkbox("Contact-hardening (PCSS)", &r.pcss);
        ImGui::SliderFloat("Softness", &r.shadowSoftness, 0.1f, 6.0f);
        ImGui::SliderFloat("Normal bias", &r.shadowNormalBias, 0.0f, 5.0f);
        ImGui::SliderFloat("Depth bias", &r.shadowDepthBias, 0.0f, 5.0f);
        ImGui::Checkbox("Stagger far cascades", &r.staggerCascades);
    }
    if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Screen space")) {
        ImGui::Checkbox("Ground-truth AO", &r.ssao);
        ImGui::SliderFloat("AO radius", &r.aoRadius, 0.1f, 5.0f, "%.2f m");
        ImGui::SliderFloat("AO strength", &r.aoIntensity, 0.2f, 3.0f);
        ImGui::SliderInt("AO slices", &r.aoSlices, 1, 6);
        ImGui::SliderInt("AO steps", &r.aoSteps, 2, 12);
        ImGui::Checkbox("Reflections (SSR)", &r.ssr);
        ImGui::SliderFloat("SSR max roughness", &r.ssrMaxRoughness, 0.05f, 1.0f);
        ImGui::SliderFloat("SSR thickness", &r.ssrThickness, 0.05f, 3.0f);
        ImGui::SliderInt("SSR steps", &r.ssrSteps, 8, 128);
        ImGui::Checkbox("Height fog", &r.fog);
        if (r.fog) {
            ImGui::SliderFloat("Fog density", &r.fogDensity, 0.0f, 0.05f, "%.4f", ImGuiSliderFlags_Logarithmic);
            ImGui::SliderFloat("Fog falloff", &r.fogHeightFalloff, 0.001f, 1.0f, "%.3f", ImGuiSliderFlags_Logarithmic);
            ImGui::DragFloat("Fog height", &r.fogHeight, 0.5f, -1000.0f, 1000.0f, "%.1f m");
        }
        ImGui::Checkbox("Specular anti-aliasing", &r.specularAntiAliasing);
        ImGui::Checkbox("Multi-scatter energy compensation", &r.multiScatter);
    }
    if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Level of detail")) {
        if (ImGui::SliderFloat("LOD bias", &state.lodBias, 0.1f, 8.0f, "%.2f", ImGuiSliderFlags_Logarithmic)) engine.setLodBias(state.lodBias);
        if (ImGui::SliderInt("Forced LOD", &state.forcedLod, -1, 6, state.forcedLod < 0 ? "automatic" : "LOD %d")) engine.setForcedLod(state.forcedLod);
        if (ImGui::SliderFloat("Cull threshold", &state.smallObjectThreshold, 0.0f, 0.01f, "%.4f")) engine.setSmallObjectThreshold(state.smallObjectThreshold);
        if (ImGui::IsItemHovered()) ImGui::SetTooltip("Projected radius below which objects are skipped. 0 keeps every object.");
    }
    if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Debug")) {
        int view = static_cast<int>(r.debugView);
        if (ImGui::Combo("View", &view, "Lit\0Base color\0Normals\0Roughness\0Metallic\0Ambient occlusion\0Sun shadows\0Reflections\0Lighting only\0Luminance heatmap\0Light clusters\0")) r.debugView = static_cast<DebugView>(view);
        if (ImGui::Checkbox("Hi-Z depth", &state.debugDepth)) engine.setDebugDepthMode(state.debugDepth);
        ImGui::Checkbox("Animate stress objects", &state.animateStress);
        ImGui::Checkbox("Grid", &ctx.showGrid);
        ImGui::Checkbox("Stats overlay", &ctx.showStats);
        if (ImGui::Button("Reload shaders (F9)")) engine.reloadShaders();
        for (const ShaderError& error : engine.shaderErrors()) ImGui::TextColored(ImVec4(1.0f, 0.45f, 0.4f, 1.0f), "%s\n%s", error.source.c_str(), error.message.c_str());
    }
    if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("GPU", ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_DefaultOpen)) {
        float peak = 1.0f;
        for (float v : m_gpuHistory) peak = std::max(peak, v);
        ImGui::PlotLines("##gpu", m_gpuHistory.data(), static_cast<int>(m_gpuHistory.size()), static_cast<int>(m_historyHead), std::format("{:.2f} ms", gpu).c_str(), 0.0f, peak * 1.2f, ImVec2(-1.0f, 60.0f));
        if (ImGui::BeginTable("passes", 2, ImGuiTableFlags_RowBg | ImGuiTableFlags_SizingStretchProp)) {
            for (const GpuPassTiming& pass : engine.gpuTimings()) {
                if (pass.milliseconds < 0.005) continue;
                ImGui::TableNextRow();
                ImGui::TableSetColumnIndex(0);
                ImGui::TextUnformatted(std::string(pass.name).c_str());
                ImGui::TableSetColumnIndex(1);
                const float fraction = gpu > 0.0 ? static_cast<float>(pass.milliseconds / gpu) : 0.0f;
                ImGui::ProgressBar(fraction, ImVec2(-1.0f, 0.0f), std::format("{:.3f} ms", pass.milliseconds).c_str());
            }
            ImGui::EndTable();
        }
    }
    ImGui::PopItemWidth();
    ImGui::End();
}

void LookPanel::drawWorld(EditorContext& ctx) {
    if (!ctx.selection.empty()) ctx.worldDismissed = false;
    m_worldVisible = !ctx.zen && (ctx.showWorld || (ctx.selection.empty() && ctx.showInspector && !ctx.showLook && !ctx.worldDismissed));
    if (!m_worldVisible) return;
    const ImGuiViewport* viewport = ImGui::GetMainViewport();
    const float width = 330.0f;
    float right = viewport->WorkPos.x + viewport->WorkSize.x - 12.0f;
    for (const glm::vec4& panel : ctx.panels) {
        if (panel.x + panel.z > viewport->WorkPos.x + viewport->WorkSize.x * 0.5f) right = std::min(right, panel.x - 10.0f);
    }
    const float top = ctx.rightColumnTop;
    ImGui::SetNextWindowPos(ImVec2(right - width, top), ImGuiCond_Always);
    ImGui::SetNextWindowSizeConstraints(ImVec2(width, 60.0f), ImVec2(width, std::max(200.0f, viewport->WorkPos.y + viewport->WorkSize.y - top - 44.0f)));
    ImGui::SetNextWindowBgAlpha(0.92f);
    bool open = true;
    if (!ImGui::Begin("World###WorldCard", &open, ImGuiWindowFlags_AlwaysAutoResize | ImGuiWindowFlags_NoFocusOnAppearing | ImGuiWindowFlags_NoSavedSettings | ImGuiWindowFlags_NoMove)) {
        ImGui::End();
        return;
    }
    if (!open) {
        ctx.showWorld = false;
        ctx.worldDismissed = true;
    }
    RenderSettings& r = ctx.engine.renderSettings();
    ImGui::TextDisabled("Nothing selected: editing the world. Shift+W toggles.");
    ImGui::PushItemWidth(-110.0f);
    if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Sky and sun", ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_DefaultOpen)) {
        int sky = static_cast<int>(r.sky);
        if (ImGui::Combo("Sky", &sky, "Physical atmosphere\0Gradient\0HDRI\0")) r.sky = static_cast<SkyMode>(sky);
        sunDial("worldSun", r.timeOfDay, r.sunAzimuth);
        ImGui::SliderFloat("Time of day", &r.timeOfDay, 0.0f, 24.0f, "%.2f h");
        ImGui::SliderFloat("Sun azimuth", &r.sunAzimuth, 0.0f, 360.0f, "%.0f°");
        ImGui::DragFloat("Sun", &r.sunIlluminance, 500.0f, 0.0f, 200000.0f, "%.0f lux");
        ImGui::SliderFloat("Sky brightness", &r.skyBrightness, 0.0f, 4.0f, "%.2f");
        if (r.sky == SkyMode::Physical) {
            ImGui::Checkbox("Clouds", &r.clouds);
            if (r.clouds) {
                ImGui::SameLine();
                ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(-1.0f);
                ImGui::SliderFloat("##coverage", &r.cloudCoverage, 0.0f, 1.0f, "coverage %.2f");
            }
        }
        if (r.sky == SkyMode::Gradient) {
            ImGui::ColorEdit3("Zenith", &r.gradientZenith.x);
            ImGui::ColorEdit3("Horizon", &r.gradientHorizon.x);
        }
        if (r.sky == SkyMode::Hdri) ImGui::InputTextWithHint("HDRI", "resources/sky.hdr", &r.hdriPath);
    }
    if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Light and exposure", ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_DefaultOpen)) {
        ImGui::SliderFloat("Ambient (IBL)", &r.environmentIntensity, 0.0f, 4.0f);
        ImGui::Checkbox("Auto exposure", &r.autoExposure);
        ImGui::SameLine();
        ImGui::TextDisabled("EV100 %.1f", ctx.engine.exposureEv100());
        if (r.autoExposure) {
            ImGui::SliderFloat("Compensation", &r.exposureCompensation, -5.0f, 5.0f, "%+.1f EV");
        } else {
            ImGui::SliderFloat("EV100", &r.manualEV100, -6.0f, 18.0f, "%.1f");
        }
        int op = static_cast<int>(r.tonemapper);
        if (ImGui::Combo("Tonemapper", &op, "AgX\0AgX punchy\0ACES\0Khronos PBR Neutral\0Reinhard\0Linear\0")) r.tonemapper = static_cast<Tonemapper>(op);
    }
    if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Atmosphere and fog")) {
        ImGui::Checkbox("Height fog", &r.fog);
        if (r.fog) {
            ImGui::SliderFloat("Density", &r.fogDensity, 0.0f, 0.05f, "%.4f", ImGuiSliderFlags_Logarithmic);
            ImGui::DragFloat("Height", &r.fogHeight, 0.5f, -1000.0f, 1000.0f, "%.1f m");
        }
        if (r.sky == SkyMode::Physical) {
            ImGui::SliderFloat("Haze", &r.mieScale, 0.0f, 10.0f, "%.2f", ImGuiSliderFlags_Logarithmic);
            ImGui::SliderFloat("Aerial distance", &r.aerialDistanceScale, 0.0f, 20.0f);
        }
    }
    if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Effects")) {
        ImGui::Checkbox("Shadows", &r.shadows);
        ImGui::SameLine(120.0f);
        ImGui::Checkbox("AO", &r.ssao);
        ImGui::SameLine(200.0f);
        ImGui::Checkbox("SSR", &r.ssr);
        ImGui::Checkbox("Bloom", &r.bloom);
        ImGui::SameLine(120.0f);
        ImGui::Checkbox("FXAA", &r.fxaa);
    }
    ImGui::PopItemWidth();
    if (ImGui::SmallButton("All render settings (L)")) ctx.showLook = true;
    ImGui::End();
}

Editor/Palette.cppm
```
module;

#include <cstdint>
#include <functional>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

export module Editor.palette;

import Editor.context;
import Editor.commands;
import Engine.Render.entity;

export struct PromptRequest {
    std::string title;
    std::string initial;
    std::string hint;
    std::vector<std::string> suggestions;
    std::function<void(const std::string&)> accept;
};

export class Palette {
  public:
    void open(EditorContext& ctx, std::string seed = {});
    void prompt(PromptRequest request);
    void draw(EditorContext& ctx, CommandRegistry& commands);
    [[nodiscard]] bool isOpen() const { return m_open; }

  private:
    struct Result {
        enum class Kind { Command, Entity, Calculator, Suggestion, Help };
        Kind kind = Kind::Command;
        std::string title;
        std::string detail;
        std::string shortcut;
        std::string commandId;
        EntityHandle entity;
        float score = 0.0f;
        bool enabled = true;
    };

    void rebuild(EditorContext& ctx, CommandRegistry& commands);
    void scanEntities(EditorContext& ctx);
    void activate(EditorContext& ctx, CommandRegistry& commands, const Result& result);

    bool m_open = false;
    bool m_focus = false;
    std::string m_text;
    std::string m_builtFor = "\x01";
    std::vector<Result> m_results;
    std::vector<Result> m_entityResults;
    int m_cursor = 0;
    Entity m_scanCursor = 0;
    bool m_scanDone = true;
    std::string m_scanPattern;
    bool m_promptMode = false;
    PromptRequest m_prompt;
    bool m_scrollToCursor = false;
};

```

Editor/Palette/Palette.cpp
```
module;

#include <imgui.h>
#include <imgui_stdlib.h>

#include <algorithm>
#include <cctype>
#include <cstdint>
#include <format>
#include <optional>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

module Editor.palette;

import Editor.context;
import Editor.commands;
import Engine.engine;
import Engine.World;
import Engine.Render.entity;
import Engine.glm;

namespace {

constexpr size_t kEntityBudget = 400000;
constexpr size_t kEntityResults = 60;

std::string trim(const std::string& s) {
    size_t a = 0, b = s.size();
    while (a < b && std::isspace(static_cast<unsigned char>(s[a]))) ++a;
    while (b > a && std::isspace(static_cast<unsigned char>(s[b - 1]))) --b;
    return s.substr(a, b - a);
}

ImU32 kindColor(int kind) {
    switch (kind) {
        case 1: return IM_COL32(110, 200, 255, 255);
        case 2: return IM_COL32(140, 230, 140, 255);
        case 3: return IM_COL32(255, 200, 110, 255);
        default: return IM_COL32(255, 176, 66, 255);
    }
}

}  // namespace

void Palette::open(EditorContext&, std::string seed) {
    m_open = true;
    m_focus = true;
    m_promptMode = false;
    m_text = std::move(seed);
    m_builtFor = "\x01";
    m_cursor = 0;
}

void Palette::prompt(PromptRequest request) {
    m_prompt = std::move(request);
    m_promptMode = true;
    m_open = true;
    m_focus = true;
    m_text = m_prompt.initial;
    m_builtFor = "\x01";
    m_cursor = 0;
}

void Palette::scanEntities(EditorContext& ctx) {
    if (m_scanDone) return;
    World& world = ctx.world;
    const size_t slots = world.database().transforms.size();
    size_t budget = kEntityBudget;
    const bool numeric = !m_scanPattern.empty() && m_scanPattern[0] == '#';
    const long long wanted = numeric ? std::atoll(m_scanPattern.c_str() + 1) : -1;
    if (numeric) {
        const EntityHandle e = wanted >= 0 ? world.handleOf(static_cast<Entity>(wanted)) : NULL_ENTITY;
        if (!e.isNull()) m_entityResults.push_back({Result::Kind::Entity, displayName(world, e), meshLabel(ctx.engine, world.getMesh(e)), "", "", e, 100.0f});
        m_scanDone = true;
        return;
    }
    while (m_scanCursor < slots && budget-- > 0) {
        const EntityHandle e = world.handleOf(m_scanCursor++);
        if (e.isNull()) continue;
        const std::string& name = world.getName(e);
        if (name.empty()) continue;
        const float score = fuzzyScore(m_scanPattern, name);
        if (score <= 0.0f) continue;
        m_entityResults.push_back({Result::Kind::Entity, name, meshLabel(ctx.engine, world.getMesh(e)) + (world.getTag(e).empty() ? "" : "  #" + world.getTag(e)), "", "", e, score * 0.9f});
        if (m_entityResults.size() > kEntityResults * 4) {
            std::sort(m_entityResults.begin(), m_entityResults.end(), [](const Result& a, const Result& b) { return a.score > b.score; });
            m_entityResults.resize(kEntityResults);
        }
    }
    if (m_scanCursor >= slots) m_scanDone = true;
}

void Palette::rebuild(EditorContext& ctx, CommandRegistry& commands) {
    const bool changed = m_builtFor != m_text;
    std::string text = trim(m_text);
    if (changed) {
        m_builtFor = m_text;
        m_cursor = 0;
        m_entityResults.clear();
        m_scanCursor = 0;
        m_scanDone = true;
    }
    m_results.clear();

    if (m_promptMode) {
        m_results.push_back({Result::Kind::Suggestion, text.empty() ? m_prompt.hint : text, "Enter to accept", "", "", NULL_ENTITY, 1000.0f});
        for (const std::string& s : m_prompt.suggestions) {
            const float score = text.empty() ? 1.0f : fuzzyScore(text, s);
            if (score > 0.0f && s != text) m_results.push_back({Result::Kind::Suggestion, s, "", "", "", NULL_ENTITY, score});
        }
        std::stable_sort(m_results.begin() + 1, m_results.end(), [](const Result& a, const Result& b) { return a.score > b.score; });
        return;
    }

    enum class Mode { All, Commands, Entities, Calc, Help };
    Mode mode = Mode::All;
    if (!text.empty() && text[0] == '>') mode = Mode::Commands;
    if (!text.empty() && text[0] == '@') mode = Mode::Entities;
    if (!text.empty() && text[0] == '=') mode = Mode::Calc;
    if (!text.empty() && text[0] == '?') mode = Mode::Help;
    const std::string pattern = mode == Mode::All ? text : trim(text.substr(std::min<size_t>(1, text.size())));

    if (mode == Mode::Calc || mode == Mode::All) {
        if (const std::optional<double> value = evaluateExpression(pattern); value && (mode == Mode::Calc || pattern.find_first_of("+-*/^(") != std::string::npos)) {
            m_results.push_back({Result::Kind::Calculator, std::format("= {:.6g}", *value), "Enter copies the result", "", "", NULL_ENTITY, 1000.0f});
        }
        if (mode == Mode::Calc) return;
    }
    if (mode == Mode::Help) {
        const char* tips[] = {"Type to fuzzy-search every command, e.g. 'dup' finds Duplicate", "> filters commands only, @ finds entities by name or #index", "= evaluates math: '=2*pi*3'", "Hold Space for the radial menu, tap for this palette",
                              "G/R/S then X/Y/Z then a number: exact transforms without the mouse", "Ctrl+1..9 stores a selection, 1..9 recalls it", "Ctrl+Alt+1..9 stores a camera view, Alt+1..9 recalls it", "[ and ] walk siblings, Shift+[ parent, Shift+] child"};
        for (const char* tip : tips) m_results.push_back({Result::Kind::Help, tip, "", "", "", NULL_ENTITY, 1.0f});
        return;
    }
    if (mode == Mode::All || mode == Mode::Commands) {
        for (const Command& command : commands.all()) {
            if (!command.palette) continue;
            const std::string haystack = command.category + " " + command.title;
            float score = pattern.empty() ? 1.0f : std::max(fuzzyScore(pattern, command.title) * 1.2f, fuzzyScore(pattern, haystack));
            if (score <= 0.0f) continue;
            const uint64_t last = commands.lastUse(command.id);
            if (last > 0) score += 2.0f + static_cast<float>(std::min<uint32_t>(commands.uses(command.id), 20)) * 0.25f;
            if (pattern.empty() && last > 0) score += static_cast<float>(last) * 1e-3f + 50.0f;
            const bool enabled = !command.enabled || command.enabled();
            std::string title = command.title;
            if (command.checked) title += command.checked() ? "  (on)" : "  (off)";
            m_results.push_back({Result::Kind::Command, title, command.category, command.chords.empty() ? "" : chordLabel(command.chords.front()), command.id, NULL_ENTITY, enabled ? score : score * 0.3f, enabled});
        }
    }
    if ((mode == Mode::All && pattern.size() >= 2) || mode == Mode::Entities) {
        if (changed) {
            m_scanPattern = pattern;
            m_scanDone = pattern.empty();
        }
        scanEntities(ctx);
        for (const Result& r : m_entityResults) m_results.push_back(r);
    }
    std::stable_sort(m_results.begin(), m_results.end(), [](const Result& a, const Result& b) { return a.score > b.score; });
    if (m_results.size() > 200) m_results.resize(200);
}

void Palette::activate(EditorContext& ctx, CommandRegistry& commands, const Result& result) {
    switch (result.kind) {
        case Result::Kind::Command:
            m_open = false;
            if (!commands.run(result.commandId)) ctx.toast(std::format("'{}' is not available right now", result.title), ToastKind::Warning);
            break;
        case Result::Kind::Entity: {
            m_open = false;
            ctx.selection.set(result.entity);
            const glm::vec4 b = ctx.world.getWorldBounds(result.entity);
            flyTo(ctx, b.w > 0.0f ? b : glm::vec4(ctx.world.getWorldPosition(result.entity), 1.0f));
            break;
        }
        case Result::Kind::Calculator: {
            const std::string value = result.title.substr(2);
            ImGui::SetClipboardText(value.c_str());
            ctx.toast("Copied " + value, ToastKind::Success);
            m_open = false;
            break;
        }
        case Result::Kind::Suggestion: {
            m_open = false;
            const std::string value = &result == &m_results.front() ? trim(m_text) : result.title;
            if (m_prompt.accept && !value.empty()) m_prompt.accept(value);
            m_promptMode = false;
            break;
        }
        case Result::Kind::Help: m_text = result.title.find("> ") != std::string::npos ? ">" : ""; break;
    }
}

void Palette::draw(EditorContext& ctx, CommandRegistry& commands) {
    if (ctx.openPalette) {
        open(ctx, ctx.paletteSeed);
        ctx.openPalette = false;
        ctx.paletteSeed.clear();
    }
    if (!m_open) return;
    rebuild(ctx, commands);

    const ImGuiViewport* viewport = ImGui::GetMainViewport();
    const float width = std::min(680.0f, viewport->WorkSize.x - 40.0f);
    ImGui::SetNextWindowPos(ImVec2(viewport->WorkPos.x + (viewport->WorkSize.x - width) * 0.5f, viewport->WorkPos.y + viewport->WorkSize.y * 0.14f));
    ImGui::SetNextWindowSize(ImVec2(width, 0.0f));
    ImGui::SetNextWindowBgAlpha(0.97f);
    ImGui::PushStyleVar(ImGuiStyleVar_WindowRounding, 12.0f);
    ImGui::PushStyleVar(ImGuiStyleVar_WindowPadding, ImVec2(10.0f, 10.0f));
    const ImGuiWindowFlags flags = ImGuiWindowFlags_NoTitleBar | ImGuiWindowFlags_NoResize | ImGuiWindowFlags_NoMove | ImGuiWindowFlags_AlwaysAutoResize | ImGuiWindowFlags_NoSavedSettings | ImGuiWindowFlags_NoDocking;
    ImGui::Begin("##palette", nullptr, flags);
    ImGui::SetWindowFocus();
    if (m_promptMode) {
        ImGui::TextColored(ImVec4(1.0f, 0.72f, 0.3f, 1.0f), "%s", m_prompt.title.c_str());
    }
    if (m_focus || !ImGui::IsAnyItemActive()) {
        ImGui::SetKeyboardFocusHere();
        m_focus = false;
    }
    ImGui::PushStyleVar(ImGuiStyleVar_FramePadding, ImVec2(10.0f, 8.0f));
    ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(-1.0f);
    ImGui::InputTextWithHint("##query", m_promptMode ? m_prompt.hint.c_str() : "Search commands and entities     >commands  @entities  =math  ?tips", &m_text);
    ImGui::PopStyleVar();

    const int count = static_cast<int>(m_results.size());
    if (ImGui::IsKeyPressed(ImGuiKey_DownArrow) || (ImGui::IsKeyPressed(ImGuiKey_Tab) && !ImGui::GetIO().KeyShift)) {
        m_cursor = count > 0 ? (m_cursor + 1) % count : 0;
        m_scrollToCursor = true;
    }
    if (ImGui::IsKeyPressed(ImGuiKey_UpArrow) || (ImGui::IsKeyPressed(ImGuiKey_Tab) && ImGui::GetIO().KeyShift)) {
        m_cursor = count > 0 ? (m_cursor + count - 1) % count : 0;
        m_scrollToCursor = true;
    }
    if (ImGui::IsKeyPressed(ImGuiKey_PageDown)) m_cursor = std::min(m_cursor + 8, std::max(count - 1, 0));
    if (ImGui::IsKeyPressed(ImGuiKey_PageUp)) m_cursor = std::max(m_cursor - 8, 0);
    m_cursor = std::clamp(m_cursor, 0, std::max(count - 1, 0));

    const float rowHeight = ImGui::GetTextLineHeight() * 1.9f;
    const float listHeight = std::min(static_cast<float>(std::max(count, 1)) * rowHeight, rowHeight * 11.0f);
    ImGui::BeginChild("results", ImVec2(0.0f, listHeight + 4.0f));
    ImDrawList* draw = ImGui::GetWindowDrawList();
    int clicked = -1;
    for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
        const Result& r = m_results[i];
        ImGui::PushID(i);
        const ImVec2 start = ImGui::GetCursorScreenPos();
        const float w = ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail().x;
        if (ImGui::Selectable("##item", i == m_cursor, 0, ImVec2(w, rowHeight - ImGui::GetStyle().ItemSpacing.y))) clicked = i;
        if (ImGui::IsItemHovered() && ImGui::GetIO().MouseDelta.x != 0.0f) m_cursor = i;
        if (i == m_cursor && m_scrollToCursor) {
            ImGui::SetScrollHereY(0.5f);
            m_scrollToCursor = false;
        }
        const float y = start.y + (rowHeight - ImGui::GetStyle().ItemSpacing.y - ImGui::GetFontSize()) * 0.5f;
        const int kind = static_cast<int>(r.kind);
        draw->AddCircleFilled(ImVec2(start.x + 10.0f, y + ImGui::GetFontSize() * 0.5f), 3.5f, kindColor(kind));
        draw->AddText(ImVec2(start.x + 22.0f, y), r.enabled ? IM_COL32(236, 238, 242, 255) : IM_COL32(120, 124, 132, 255), r.title.c_str());
        float right = start.x + w - 8.0f;
        if (!r.shortcut.empty()) {
            const ImVec2 size = ImGui::CalcTextSize(r.shortcut.c_str());
            const ImVec2 lo(right - size.x - 12.0f, y - 2.0f);
            draw->AddRectFilled(lo, ImVec2(right, y + size.y + 2.0f), IM_COL32(255, 255, 255, 18), 5.0f);
            draw->AddRect(lo, ImVec2(right, y + size.y + 2.0f), IM_COL32(255, 255, 255, 30), 5.0f);
            draw->AddText(ImVec2(lo.x + 6.0f, y), IM_COL32(210, 214, 222, 255), r.shortcut.c_str());
            right = lo.x - 10.0f;
        }
        if (!r.detail.empty()) {
            const ImVec2 size = ImGui::CalcTextSize(r.detail.c_str());
            const float titleEnd = start.x + 22.0f + ImGui::CalcTextSize(r.title.c_str()).x + 16.0f;
            if (right - size.x > titleEnd) draw->AddText(ImVec2(right - size.x, y), IM_COL32(120, 128, 140, 255), r.detail.c_str());
        }
        ImGui::PopID();
    }
    if (count == 0) ImGui::TextDisabled(m_scanDone ? "  No matches" : "  Searching...");
    ImGui::EndChild();
    if (!m_promptMode) {
        ImGui::TextDisabled("Enter run   Up/Down move   Esc close   %s", m_scanDone ? "" : "scanning entities...");
    }
    ImGui::End();
    ImGui::PopStyleVar(2);

    if (clicked >= 0) {
        activate(ctx, commands, m_results[clicked]);
    } else if ((ImGui::IsKeyPressed(ImGuiKey_Enter) || ImGui::IsKeyPressed(ImGuiKey_KeypadEnter)) && count > 0) {
        activate(ctx, commands, m_results[m_cursor]);
    }
    if (ImGui::IsKeyPressed(ImGuiKey_Escape)) {
        m_open = false;
        m_promptMode = false;
    }
}

Editor/Commands/Commands.cpp
```
module;

#include <imgui.h>

#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

module Editor.commands;

std::string chordLabel(const Chord& chord) {
    std::string out;
    if (chord.ctrl) out += "Ctrl+";
    if (chord.shift) out += "Shift+";
    if (chord.alt) out += "Alt+";
    const char* name = ImGui::GetKeyName(chord.key);
    std::string keyName = name ? name : "?";
    if (keyName == "Space") keyName = "Space";
    if (keyName == "LeftBracket") keyName = "[";
    if (keyName == "RightBracket") keyName = "]";
    if (keyName == "Period") keyName = ".";
    if (keyName == "Comma") keyName = ",";
    if (keyName == "Slash") keyName = "/";
    if (keyName == "GraveAccent") keyName = "`";
    if (keyName == "Minus") keyName = "-";
    if (keyName == "Equal") keyName = "=";
    if (keyName == "Delete") keyName = "Del";
    if (keyName == "Escape") keyName = "Esc";
    if (keyName == "UpArrow") keyName = "Up";
    if (keyName == "DownArrow") keyName = "Down";
    if (keyName == "LeftArrow") keyName = "Left";
    if (keyName == "RightArrow") keyName = "Right";
    if (keyName == "PageUp") keyName = "PgUp";
    if (keyName == "PageDown") keyName = "PgDn";
    return out + keyName;
}

Command& CommandRegistry::add(Command command) {
    const auto it = m_index.find(command.id);
    if (it != m_index.end()) {
        m_commands[it->second] = std::move(command);
        return m_commands[it->second];
    }
    m_index.emplace(command.id, m_commands.size());
    m_commands.push_back(std::move(command));
    return m_commands.back();
}

const Command* CommandRegistry::find(const std::string& id) const {
    const auto it = m_index.find(id);
    return it == m_index.end() ? nullptr : &m_commands[it->second];
}

bool CommandRegistry::run(const std::string& id) {
    const auto it = m_index.find(id);
    if (it == m_index.end()) return false;
    Command& command = m_commands[it->second];
    if (command.enabled && !command.enabled()) return false;
    if (!command.run) return false;
    ++m_uses[id];
    m_lastUse[id] = ++m_clock;
    const auto fn = command.run;
    fn();
    return true;
}

void CommandRegistry::processShortcuts(bool viewportKeys) {
    const ImGuiIO& io = ImGui::GetIO();
    if (io.WantTextInput) return;
    const bool ctrlDown = io.KeyCtrl;
    const bool shiftDown = io.KeyShift;
    const bool altDown = io.KeyAlt;
    std::vector<std::string> fired;
    for (const Command& command : m_commands) {
        if (command.scope == CommandScope::Viewport && !viewportKeys) continue;
        for (const Chord& chord : command.chords) {
            if (chord.key == ImGuiKey_None) continue;
            if (chord.ctrl != ctrlDown || chord.shift != shiftDown || chord.alt != altDown) continue;
            if (!ImGui::IsKeyPressed(chord.key, command.repeat)) continue;
            fired.push_back(command.id);
            break;
        }
    }
    for (const std::string& id : fired) run(id);
}

std::string CommandRegistry::shortcut(const std::string& id) const {
    const Command* command = find(id);
    if (!command || command->chords.empty()) return {};
    return chordLabel(command->chords.front());
}

uint32_t CommandRegistry::uses(const std::string& id) const {
    const auto it = m_uses.find(id);
    return it == m_uses.end() ? 0 : it->second;
}

uint64_t CommandRegistry::lastUse(const std::string& id) const {
    const auto it = m_lastUse.find(id);
    return it == m_lastUse.end() ? 0 : it->second;
}

```

Editor/Viewport/Viewport.cpp
```
module;

#include <imgui.h>
#include <ImGuizmo.h>

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstring>
#include <format>
#include <optional>
#include <vector>

module Editor.viewport;

import Editor.context;
import Engine.engine;
import Engine.World;
import Engine.camera;
import Engine.input;
import Engine.Render.entity;
import Engine.glm;

namespace {

constexpr float kClickSlop = 5.0f;

bool hasIcon(World& world, EntityHandle e) { return world.get<LightComponent>(e) != nullptr || world.get<CameraComponent>(e) != nullptr; }

glm::vec3 cameraRight(const Camera& camera) {
    return glm::normalize(glm::cross(cameraForward(camera), glm::vec3(0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f)));
}

void orientTowards(Camera& camera, const glm::vec3& direction) {
    const glm::vec3 d = glm::normalize(direction);
    camera.setOrientation(glm::degrees(std::atan2(d.z, d.x)), glm::degrees(std::asin(std::clamp(d.y, -1.0f, 1.0f))));
}

}  // namespace

glm::vec3 Viewport::pivotFor(EditorContext& ctx) const {
    const auto top = ctx.topLevelSelection();
    if (!top.empty()) {
        glm::vec3 sum(0.0f);
