```
        for (EntityHandle e : top) sum += ctx.world.getWorldPosition(e);
        return sum / static_cast<float>(top.size());
    }
    if (ctx.cursorOnSurface) return ctx.cursor;
    const Camera& camera = ctx.world.camera();
    return camera.getPosition() + cameraForward(camera) * 10.0f;
}

void Viewport::cancelInteractions(EditorContext& ctx) {
    m_pressing = false;
    m_boxing = false;
    m_orbiting = false;
    m_panning = false;
    if (ctx.flying) {
        ctx.flying = false;
        ctx.engine.setMouseCaptured(false);
    }
}

void Viewport::update(EditorContext& ctx, float dt, bool mouseFree) {
    const ImGuiIO& io = ImGui::GetIO();
    if (ImGui::IsMousePosValid(&io.MousePos) && std::isfinite(io.MousePos.x) && std::isfinite(io.MousePos.y)) ctx.mouse = glm::vec2(io.MousePos.x, io.MousePos.y);
    ctx.viewportHovered = mouseFree;
    updateFlight(ctx, dt);
    updateFly(ctx, dt);
    if (!ctx.flying) updateOrbitPan(ctx);
    if (!ctx.flying && !m_orbiting && !m_panning) {
        updateHover(ctx, dt);
        if (!ctx.modalActive) handleClicks(ctx, mouseFree);
    }

    if (mouseFree && !ctx.flying && !m_orbiting && io.MouseWheel != 0.0f && !ctx.modalActive) {
        Camera& camera = ctx.world.camera();
        const Ray ray = ctx.engine.screenRay(ctx.mouse.x, ctx.mouse.y);
        if (camera.isOrthographic()) {
            const float factor = std::pow(io.KeyShift ? 0.7f : 0.87f, io.MouseWheel);
            const glm::vec3 forward = cameraForward(camera);
            glm::vec3 offset = ray.origin - camera.getPosition();
            offset -= forward * glm::dot(offset, forward);
            camera.setOrthoHeight(std::clamp(camera.getOrthoHeight() * factor, 0.01f, 100000.0f));
            camera.setPos(camera.getPosition() + offset * (1.0f - factor));
        } else {
            const float distance = ctx.cursorOnSurface ? glm::length(ctx.cursor - camera.getPosition()) : ctx.flySpeed * 2.0f;
            const float step = std::clamp(distance * 0.15f, 0.05f, 5000.0f) * io.MouseWheel * (io.KeyShift ? 3.0f : 1.0f);
            camera.setPos(camera.getPosition() + ray.direction * step);
        }
        ctx.flight.active = false;
    }
}

void Viewport::updateFly(EditorContext& ctx, float dt) {
    const ImGuiIO& io = ImGui::GetIO();
    const bool rightDown = ImGui::IsMouseDown(ImGuiMouseButton_Right);
    if (!ctx.flying) {
        if (ImGui::IsMouseClicked(ImGuiMouseButton_Right) && ctx.viewportHovered && !ctx.modalActive) {
            ctx.flying = true;
            ctx.flight.active = false;
            ctx.world.camera().setOrthographic(false);
            ctx.engine.setMouseCaptured(true);
        }
        return;
    }
    if (!rightDown) {
        ctx.flying = false;
        ctx.engine.setMouseCaptured(false);
        return;
    }
    Camera& camera = ctx.world.camera();
    const glm::vec2 delta = InputManager::GetMouseDelta();
    camera.processMouseMovement(delta.x, -delta.y);
    if (io.MouseWheel != 0.0f) ctx.flySpeed = std::clamp(ctx.flySpeed * std::pow(1.2f, io.MouseWheel), 0.1f, 5000.0f);
    float speed = ctx.flySpeed * (io.KeyShift ? 4.0f : 1.0f) * (io.KeyCtrl ? 0.25f : 1.0f) * dt;
    const glm::vec3 forward = cameraForward(camera);
    const glm::vec3 right = cameraRight(camera);
    glm::vec3 move(0.0f);
    if (ImGui::IsKeyDown(ImGuiKey_W)) move += forward;
    if (ImGui::IsKeyDown(ImGuiKey_S)) move -= forward;
    if (ImGui::IsKeyDown(ImGuiKey_D)) move += right;
    if (ImGui::IsKeyDown(ImGuiKey_A)) move -= right;
    if (ImGui::IsKeyDown(ImGuiKey_E)) move += glm::vec3(0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f);
    if (ImGui::IsKeyDown(ImGuiKey_Q)) move -= glm::vec3(0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f);
    if (glm::dot(move, move) > 0.0f) camera.setPos(camera.getPosition() + glm::normalize(move) * speed);
    ctx.modeLabel = "FLY";
    ctx.hint("WASD", "move");
    ctx.hint("Q/E", "down/up");
    ctx.hint("Wheel", std::format("speed {:.1f}", ctx.flySpeed));
    ctx.hint("Shift/Ctrl", "fast/slow");
}

void Viewport::updateOrbitPan(EditorContext& ctx) {
    const ImGuiIO& io = ImGui::GetIO();
    Camera& camera = ctx.world.camera();
    if (!m_orbiting && !m_panning && ctx.viewportHovered && !ctx.modalActive) {
        const bool orbitStart = (ImGui::IsMouseClicked(ImGuiMouseButton_Left) && io.KeyAlt) || (ImGui::IsMouseClicked(ImGuiMouseButton_Middle) && !io.KeyShift && io.KeyAlt);
        const bool panStart = ImGui::IsMouseClicked(ImGuiMouseButton_Middle) && !io.KeyAlt;
        if (orbitStart || panStart) {
            m_orbitPivot = pivotFor(ctx);
            m_orbitDistance = std::max(glm::length(camera.getPosition() - m_orbitPivot), 0.5f);
            m_orbiting = orbitStart;
            m_panning = panStart;
            ctx.flight.active = false;
            if (orbitStart && ctx.autoOrtho && camera.isOrthographic()) {
                camera.setOrthographic(false);
                ctx.autoOrtho = false;
            }
        }
    }
    if (m_orbiting) {
        if (!ImGui::IsMouseDown(ImGuiMouseButton_Left) && !ImGui::IsMouseDown(ImGuiMouseButton_Middle)) {
            m_orbiting = false;
            return;
        }
        camera.processMouseMovement(io.MouseDelta.x * 2.5f, -io.MouseDelta.y * 2.5f);
        camera.setPos(m_orbitPivot - cameraForward(camera) * m_orbitDistance);
        ctx.modeLabel = "ORBIT";
        ctx.hint("Drag", "orbit around selection");
    }
    if (m_panning) {
        if (!ImGui::IsMouseDown(ImGuiMouseButton_Middle)) {
            m_panning = false;
            return;
        }
        const glm::vec3 right = cameraRight(camera);
        const glm::vec3 up = glm::normalize(glm::cross(right, cameraForward(camera)));
        const glm::vec2 size = ctx.engine.windowSize();
        const float unitsPerPixel = (camera.isOrthographic() ? camera.getOrthoHeight() : 2.0f * m_orbitDistance * std::tan(glm::radians(camera.getFov()) * 0.5f)) / std::max(size.y, 1.0f);
        camera.setPos(camera.getPosition() + (-right * io.MouseDelta.x + up * io.MouseDelta.y) * unitsPerPixel);
        ctx.modeLabel = "PAN";
        ctx.hint("Drag", "pan");
    }
}

void Viewport::updateHover(EditorContext& ctx, float dt) {
    m_hoverTimer += dt;
    const bool moved = glm::distance(ctx.mouse, m_lastHoverMouse) > 0.5f;
    if (!ctx.viewportHovered || ctx.modalActive || ImGuizmo::IsUsing()) {
        ctx.hovered = NULL_ENTITY;
        return;
    }
    if (!moved && m_hoverTimer < 0.25f) return;
    if (m_hoverTimer < 0.04f) return;
    m_hoverTimer = 0.0f;
    m_lastHoverMouse = ctx.mouse;
    const Ray ray = ctx.engine.screenRay(ctx.mouse.x, ctx.mouse.y);
    const std::optional<RayHit> hit = ctx.engine.pick(ctx.mouse.x, ctx.mouse.y);
    ctx.cursorValid = true;
    ctx.hovered = hit ? hit->entity : NULL_ENTITY;
    if (hit) {
        if (const std::optional<glm::vec3> point = surfacePoint(ctx, ray, *hit)) {
            ctx.cursor = *point;
            ctx.cursorOnSurface = true;
            return;
        }
    }
    ctx.cursorOnSurface = false;
    if (ray.direction.y < -1e-4f) {
        const float t = -ray.origin.y / ray.direction.y;
        if (t > 0.0f && t < 5000.0f) {
            ctx.cursor = ray.origin + ray.direction * t;
            ctx.cursorOnSurface = true;
            return;
        }
    }
    ctx.cursor = ray.origin + ray.direction * 10.0f;
}

void Viewport::handleClicks(EditorContext& ctx, bool mouseFree) {
    const ImGuiIO& io = ImGui::GetIO();
    const bool gizmoBusy = ImGuizmo::IsOver() || ImGuizmo::IsUsing();
    if (ImGui::IsMouseClicked(ImGuiMouseButton_Left) && mouseFree && !io.KeyAlt && !gizmoBusy) {
        m_pressing = true;
        m_boxing = false;
        m_pressAt = ctx.mouse;
    }
    if (!m_pressing) return;
    if (ImGui::IsMouseDown(ImGuiMouseButton_Left)) {
        if (!m_boxing && glm::distance(ctx.mouse, m_pressAt) > kClickSlop) m_boxing = true;
        if (m_boxing) {
            ctx.modeLabel = "SELECT";
            ctx.hint("Shift", "add");
            ctx.hint("Ctrl", "toggle");
        }
        return;
    }
    m_pressing = false;
    if (m_boxing) {
        finishBox(ctx);
        m_boxing = false;
        return;
    }
    EntityHandle picked = iconAt(ctx.mouse);
    if (picked.isNull()) {
        const std::optional<RayHit> hit = ctx.engine.pick(ctx.mouse.x, ctx.mouse.y);
        picked = hit ? hit->entity : NULL_ENTITY;
    }
    const double now = ImGui::GetTime();
    const bool doubleClick = picked == m_lastClicked && !picked.isNull() && now - m_lastClickTime < 0.35;
    m_lastClickTime = now;
    m_lastClicked = picked;
    if (doubleClick) {
        const glm::vec4 bounds = ctx.world.getWorldBounds(picked);
        flyTo(ctx, bounds.w > 0.0f ? bounds : glm::vec4(ctx.world.getWorldPosition(picked), 1.0f));
        return;
    }
    if (picked.isNull()) {
        if (!io.KeyShift && !io.KeyCtrl) ctx.selection.clear();
        return;
    }
    if (io.KeyCtrl) {
        ctx.selection.toggle(picked);
    } else if (io.KeyShift) {
        ctx.selection.remove(picked);
        ctx.selection.add(picked);
    } else {
        ctx.selection.set(picked);
    }
}

void Viewport::finishBox(EditorContext& ctx) {
    const ImGuiIO& io = ImGui::GetIO();
    const glm::vec2 lo = glm::min(m_pressAt, ctx.mouse);
    const glm::vec2 hi = glm::max(m_pressAt, ctx.mouse);
    constexpr size_t kLimit = 100000;
    std::vector<EntityHandle> inside;
    const std::vector<EntityHandle> candidates = ctx.world.queryFrustum(ctx.world.camera());
    for (EntityHandle e : candidates) {
        const std::optional<glm::vec2> screen = ctx.engine.worldToScreen(ctx.world.getWorldPosition(e));
        if (!screen) continue;
        if (screen->x < lo.x || screen->y < lo.y || screen->x > hi.x || screen->y > hi.y) continue;
        inside.push_back(e);
        if (inside.size() >= kLimit) break;
    }
    if (!io.KeyShift && !io.KeyCtrl) ctx.selection.clear();
    for (EntityHandle e : inside) {
        if (io.KeyCtrl) {
            ctx.selection.toggle(e);
        } else {
            ctx.selection.add(e);
        }
    }
    if (inside.size() >= kLimit) ctx.toast(std::format("Box selection capped at {} entities", kLimit), ToastKind::Warning);
}

void Viewport::drawGrid(EditorContext& ctx) {
    if (!ctx.showGrid) return;
    const Camera& camera = ctx.world.camera();
    const glm::vec3 eye = camera.getPosition();
    const float height = std::max(std::abs(eye.y), 1.0f);
    const float spacing = std::pow(10.0f, std::floor(std::log10(height)));
    constexpr int kHalf = 40;
    const float cx = std::round(eye.x / spacing) * spacing;
    const float cz = std::round(eye.z / spacing) * spacing;
    const float extent = spacing * kHalf;
    for (int i = -kHalf; i <= kHalf; ++i) {
        const float x = cx + static_cast<float>(i) * spacing;
        const float z = cz + static_cast<float>(i) * spacing;
        const bool major = static_cast<long long>(std::round(x / spacing)) % 10 == 0;
        const bool majorZ = static_cast<long long>(std::round(z / spacing)) % 10 == 0;
        const glm::vec4 minor(0.55f, 0.58f, 0.64f, 0.22f);
        const glm::vec4 strong(0.70f, 0.72f, 0.78f, 0.45f);
        if (std::abs(x) > 1e-4f) ctx.engine.debugLine(glm::vec3(x, 0.0f, cz - extent), glm::vec3(x, 0.0f, cz + extent), major ? strong : minor);
        if (std::abs(z) > 1e-4f) ctx.engine.debugLine(glm::vec3(cx - extent, 0.0f, z), glm::vec3(cx + extent, 0.0f, z), majorZ ? strong : minor);
    }
    ctx.engine.debugLine(glm::vec3(cx - extent, 0.0f, 0.0f), glm::vec3(cx + extent, 0.0f, 0.0f), glm::vec4(0.95f, 0.30f, 0.30f, 0.9f));
    ctx.engine.debugLine(glm::vec3(0.0f, 0.0f, cz - extent), glm::vec3(0.0f, 0.0f, cz + extent), glm::vec4(0.30f, 0.55f, 1.00f, 0.9f));
}

void Viewport::drawSelection(EditorContext& ctx) {
    constexpr size_t kMaxBoxes = 512;
    size_t drawn = 0;
    const EntityHandle primary = ctx.primary();
    for (EntityHandle e : ctx.selection.items()) {
        if (drawn++ >= kMaxBoxes) break;
        if (!ctx.world.isAlive(e)) continue;
        const glm::vec4 b = ctx.world.getWorldBounds(e);
        if (b.w <= 0.0f && hasIcon(ctx.world, e)) continue;
        const glm::vec3 center = b.w > 0.0f ? glm::vec3(b) : ctx.world.getWorldPosition(e);
        const float r = b.w > 0.0f ? b.w * 0.58f : 0.25f;
        const glm::vec4 color = e == primary ? glm::vec4(1.0f, 0.72f, 0.28f, 1.0f) : glm::vec4(1.0f, 0.55f, 0.15f, 0.75f);
        ctx.engine.debugBox(center, glm::vec3(r), color, true);
    }
    if (ctx.world.isAlive(ctx.hovered) && !ctx.selection.contains(ctx.hovered) && !ctx.flying && !(ctx.world.getWorldBounds(ctx.hovered).w <= 0.0f && hasIcon(ctx.world, ctx.hovered))) {
        const glm::vec4 b = ctx.world.getWorldBounds(ctx.hovered);
        const glm::vec3 center = b.w > 0.0f ? glm::vec3(b) : ctx.world.getWorldPosition(ctx.hovered);
        const float distance = glm::length(center - ctx.world.camera().getPosition());
        if (b.w < distance * 0.5f) ctx.engine.debugBox(center, glm::vec3(b.w > 0.0f ? b.w * 0.58f : 0.25f), glm::vec4(1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f, 0.35f), true);
    }
    for (EntityHandle e : ctx.selection.items()) {
        if (!ctx.world.isAlive(e)) continue;
        const EntityHandle parent = ctx.world.getParent(e);
        if (!parent.isNull()) ctx.engine.debugLine(ctx.world.getWorldPosition(parent), ctx.world.getWorldPosition(e), glm::vec4(0.4f, 0.9f, 1.0f, 0.6f), true);
        if (drawn++ >= kMaxBoxes) break;
    }
}

void Viewport::drawAxisWidget(EditorContext& ctx) {
    Camera& camera = ctx.world.camera();
    const glm::vec2 size = ctx.engine.windowSize();
    glm::mat4 view = camera.getViewMatrix();
    const float widget = 96.0f;
    const ImVec2 pos(size.x - widget - 14.0f, 14.0f);
    const float distance = m_orbitDistance > 0.0f ? m_orbitDistance : 10.0f;
    const glm::mat4 before = view;
    ImGuizmo::SetDrawlist(ImGui::GetForegroundDrawList());
    ImGuizmo::ViewManipulate(&view[0][0], distance, pos, ImVec2(widget, widget), 0x00000000);
    if (std::memcmp(&before, &view, sizeof(glm::mat4)) != 0) {
        const glm::mat4 inverse = glm::inverse(view);
        camera.setPos(glm::vec3(inverse[3]));
        orientTowards(camera, -glm::vec3(inverse[2]));
        ctx.flight.active = false;
    }
}

EntityHandle Viewport::iconAt(const glm::vec2& point) const {
    EntityHandle best;
    float bestDistance = 15.0f;
    for (const Icon& icon : m_icons) {
        const float d = glm::distance(icon.at, point);
        if (d < bestDistance) {
            bestDistance = d;
            best = icon.entity;
        }
    }
    return best;
}

void Viewport::drawIcons(EditorContext& ctx) {
    m_icons.clear();
    if (ctx.zen) return;
    constexpr size_t kMaxIcons = 4096;
    World& world = ctx.world;
    const glm::vec2 size = ctx.engine.windowSize();
    const glm::vec3 eye = world.camera().getPosition();
    ImDrawList* draw = ImGui::GetBackgroundDrawList();
    const EntityHandle hoverIcon = ctx.viewportHovered ? iconAt(ctx.mouse) : NULL_ENTITY;
    auto project = [&](const glm::vec3& p) -> std::optional<glm::vec2> {
        const std::optional<glm::vec2> s = ctx.engine.worldToScreen(p);
        if (!s || s->x < -20.0f || s->y < -20.0f || s->x > size.x + 20.0f || s->y > size.y + 20.0f) return std::nullopt;
        return s;
    };
    auto frame = [&](EntityHandle e, const ImVec2& c) {
        const bool selected = ctx.selection.contains(e);
        const bool hot = e == hoverIcon || e == ctx.hovered;
        draw->AddCircleFilled(c, 13.0f, IM_COL32(18, 20, 26, 170), 24);
        if (selected) {
            draw->AddCircle(c, 14.5f, e == ctx.primary() ? IM_COL32(255, 184, 72, 255) : IM_COL32(255, 140, 40, 200), 24, 2.0f);
        } else if (hot) {
            draw->AddCircle(c, 14.5f, IM_COL32(255, 255, 255, 160), 24, 1.5f);
        }
    };
    auto screenDirection = [&](EntityHandle e, const glm::vec2& at) {
        const glm::vec3 p = world.getWorldPosition(e);
        const float scale = std::max(glm::length(p - eye) * 0.1f, 0.1f);
        const std::optional<glm::vec2> tip = ctx.engine.worldToScreen(p + world.forward(e) * scale);
        const glm::vec2 d = tip ? *tip - at : glm::vec2(0.0f, 1.0f);
        return glm::length(d) > 1e-3f ? glm::normalize(d) : glm::vec2(0.0f, 1.0f);
    };
    world.view<LightComponent>([&](EntityHandle e, LightComponent& light) {
        if (m_icons.size() >= kMaxIcons || !world.isVisible(e)) return;
        const std::optional<glm::vec2> at = project(world.getWorldPosition(e));
        if (!at) return;
        m_icons.push_back({e, *at});
        const ImVec2 c(at->x, at->y);
        frame(e, c);
        const glm::vec3 tint = glm::clamp(light.color, glm::vec3(0.0f), glm::vec3(1.0f)) * 0.75f + glm::vec3(0.25f);
        const ImU32 color = IM_COL32(static_cast<int>(tint.r * 255.0f), static_cast<int>(tint.g * 255.0f), static_cast<int>(tint.b * 255.0f), 255);
        if (light.type == LightComponent::Type::Directional) {
            draw->AddCircleFilled(c, 4.5f, color, 16);
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
                const float a = static_cast<float>(i) * 0.785398f;
                draw->AddLine(ImVec2(c.x + std::cos(a) * 6.5f, c.y + std::sin(a) * 6.5f), ImVec2(c.x + std::cos(a) * 10.0f, c.y + std::sin(a) * 10.0f), color, 1.6f);
            }
        } else if (light.type == LightComponent::Type::Spot) {
            const glm::vec2 d = screenDirection(e, *at);
            const glm::vec2 n(-d.y, d.x);
            const glm::vec2 apex = *at - d * 6.0f;
            const glm::vec2 base = *at + d * 7.0f;
            draw->AddTriangleFilled(ImVec2(apex.x, apex.y), ImVec2(base.x + n.x * 7.0f, base.y + n.y * 7.0f), ImVec2(base.x - n.x * 7.0f, base.y - n.y * 7.0f), color);
            draw->AddCircleFilled(ImVec2(apex.x, apex.y), 3.0f, color, 12);
        } else {
            draw->AddCircleFilled(ImVec2(c.x, c.y - 2.0f), 5.5f, color, 16);
            draw->AddRectFilled(ImVec2(c.x - 3.0f, c.y + 3.5f), ImVec2(c.x + 3.0f, c.y + 7.5f), IM_COL32(190, 195, 205, 255), 1.0f);
            for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
                const float a = -3.14159f + static_cast<float>(i) * 0.785398f;
                draw->AddLine(ImVec2(c.x + std::cos(a) * 7.5f, c.y - 2.0f + std::sin(a) * 7.5f), ImVec2(c.x + std::cos(a) * 10.0f, c.y - 2.0f + std::sin(a) * 10.0f), color, 1.4f);
            }
        }
        if (!ctx.selection.contains(e) || light.type == LightComponent::Type::Directional) return;
        const glm::vec3 p = world.getWorldPosition(e);
        const glm::vec4 lineColor(tint, 0.5f);
        if (light.type == LightComponent::Type::Spot) {
            const glm::vec3 f = world.forward(e);
            const glm::vec3 side = glm::normalize(glm::cross(f, std::abs(f.y) > 0.99f ? glm::vec3(1.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f) : glm::vec3(0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f)));
            const glm::vec3 up = glm::cross(side, f);
            const float length = std::min(light.range, 6.0f);
            const float radius = length * std::tan(glm::radians(light.outerConeDegrees));
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
                const float a0 = static_cast<float>(i) * 0.785398f;
                const float a1 = a0 + 0.785398f;
                const glm::vec3 r0 = p + f * length + (side * std::cos(a0) + up * std::sin(a0)) * radius;
                const glm::vec3 r1 = p + f * length + (side * std::cos(a1) + up * std::sin(a1)) * radius;
                ctx.engine.debugLine(r0, r1, lineColor, true);
                if (i % 2 == 0) ctx.engine.debugLine(p, r0, lineColor, true);
            }
            return;
        }
        const float r = std::min(light.range, 50.0f);
        for (int axis = 0; axis < 3; ++axis) {
            auto point = [&](float a) {
                glm::vec3 v(0.0f);
                v[axis] = std::cos(a) * r;
                v[(axis + 1) % 3] = std::sin(a) * r;
                return p + v;
            };
            for (int i = 0; i < 24; ++i) ctx.engine.debugLine(point(static_cast<float>(i) * 0.261799f), point(static_cast<float>(i + 1) * 0.261799f), lineColor, true);
        }
    });
    world.view<CameraComponent>([&](EntityHandle e, CameraComponent& camera) {
        if (m_icons.size() >= kMaxIcons || !world.isVisible(e)) return;
        const std::optional<glm::vec2> at = project(world.getWorldPosition(e));
        if (!at) return;
        m_icons.push_back({e, *at});
        const ImVec2 c(at->x, at->y);
        frame(e, c);
        const ImU32 color = world.getActiveCamera() == e ? IM_COL32(120, 220, 140, 255) : IM_COL32(210, 215, 225, 255);
        draw->AddRectFilled(ImVec2(c.x - 8.0f, c.y - 4.5f), ImVec2(c.x + 2.0f, c.y + 4.5f), color, 1.5f);
        draw->AddTriangleFilled(ImVec2(c.x + 2.0f, c.y), ImVec2(c.x + 8.5f, c.y - 5.0f), ImVec2(c.x + 8.5f, c.y + 5.0f), color);
        draw->AddCircleFilled(ImVec2(c.x - 5.5f, c.y - 7.0f), 2.5f, color, 10);
        draw->AddCircleFilled(ImVec2(c.x - 0.5f, c.y - 7.0f), 2.5f, color, 10);
        if (!ctx.selection.contains(e)) return;
        const glm::mat4 m = world.getWorldMatrix(e);
        const glm::vec3 p(m[3]);
        const glm::vec3 f = world.forward(e);
        const glm::vec3 right = glm::normalize(glm::vec3(m[0]));
        const glm::vec3 up = glm::normalize(glm::vec3(m[1]));
        const float length = 1.5f;
        const float h = length * std::tan(glm::radians(camera.fov) * 0.5f);
        const float w = h * 16.0f / 9.0f;
        const glm::vec3 far = p + f * length;
        const glm::vec3 corners[4] = {far + right * w + up * h, far - right * w + up * h, far - right * w - up * h, far + right * w - up * h};
        const glm::vec4 lineColor(0.85f, 0.9f, 1.0f, 0.7f);
        for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
            ctx.engine.debugLine(p, corners[i], lineColor, true);
            ctx.engine.debugLine(corners[i], corners[(i + 1) % 4], lineColor, true);
        }
        ctx.engine.debugLine(far + up * h * 1.15f + right * w * 0.4f, far + up * h * 1.6f, lineColor, true);
        ctx.engine.debugLine(far + up * h * 1.6f, far + up * h * 1.15f - right * w * 0.4f, lineColor, true);
    });
}

void Viewport::drawOverlays(EditorContext& ctx) {
    drawGrid(ctx);
    drawSelection(ctx);
    drawIcons(ctx);
    ImDrawList* draw = ImGui::GetForegroundDrawList();
    if (m_boxing) {
        const glm::vec2 lo = glm::min(m_pressAt, ctx.mouse);
        const glm::vec2 hi = glm::max(m_pressAt, ctx.mouse);
        draw->AddRectFilled(ImVec2(lo.x, lo.y), ImVec2(hi.x, hi.y), IM_COL32(255, 176, 66, 28));
        draw->AddRect(ImVec2(lo.x, lo.y), ImVec2(hi.x, hi.y), IM_COL32(255, 176, 66, 200), 0.0f, 0, 1.5f);
    }
    if (ctx.playing) {
        const glm::vec2 size = ctx.engine.windowSize();
        draw->AddRect(ImVec2(1.0f, 1.0f), ImVec2(size.x - 1.0f, size.y - 1.0f), ctx.paused ? IM_COL32(255, 200, 60, 200) : IM_COL32(80, 230, 120, 200), 0.0f, 0, 3.0f);
    }
    if (!ctx.zen) {
        drawAxisWidget(ctx);
        if (ctx.world.camera().isOrthographic()) {
            const glm::vec2 size = ctx.engine.windowSize();
            const char* label = "Orthographic  ·  Numpad 5";
            const ImVec2 textSize = ImGui::CalcTextSize(label);
            draw->AddText(ImVec2(size.x - 124.0f - textSize.x, 18.0f), IM_COL32(255, 200, 120, 220), label);
        }
    }
}

```

Editor/Inspector/Inspector.cpp
```
module;

#include <imgui.h>
#include <imgui_stdlib.h>

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstring>
#include <format>
#include <functional>
#include <limits>
#include <optional>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

module Editor.inspector;

import Editor.context;
import Engine.engine;
import Engine.World;
import Engine.History;
import Engine.Render.entity;
import Engine.Render.component;
import Engine.Render.material;
import Engine.camera;
import Engine.glm;

namespace {

glm::quat eulerToQuat(const glm::vec3& degrees) {
    const glm::vec3 r = glm::radians(degrees);
    return glm::angleAxis(r.z, glm::vec3(0.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f)) * glm::angleAxis(r.y, glm::vec3(0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f)) * glm::angleAxis(r.x, glm::vec3(1.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f));
}

glm::vec3 quatToEuler(const glm::quat& q) {
    const glm::mat3 m = glm::mat3_cast(glm::normalize(q));
    const float sy = std::clamp(-m[0][2], -1.0f, 1.0f);
    const float y = std::asin(sy);
    float x = 0.0f;
    float z = 0.0f;
    if (std::abs(sy) < 0.9999f) {
        x = std::atan2(m[1][2], m[2][2]);
        z = std::atan2(m[0][1], m[0][0]);
    } else {
        x = std::atan2(-m[2][1], m[1][1]);
    }
    return glm::degrees(glm::vec3(x, y, z));
}

glm::mat4 compose(const glm::vec3& p, const glm::quat& r, const glm::vec3& s) { return glm::translate(glm::mat4(1.0f), p) * glm::mat4_cast(r) * glm::scale(glm::mat4(1.0f), s); }

bool axisDrag(const char* label, glm::vec3& value, float speed, const char* format, bool& activated, bool& deactivated) {
    ImGui::PushID(label);
    bool changed = false;
    activated = false;
    deactivated = false;
    const float full = ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail().x;
    const float labelWidth = 64.0f;
    ImGui::AlignTextToFramePadding();
    ImGui::TextUnformatted(label);
    ImGui::SameLine(labelWidth);
    const float each = (full - labelWidth - ImGui::GetStyle().ItemInnerSpacing.x * 2.0f) / 3.0f;
    const ImU32 colors[3] = {IM_COL32(230, 80, 80, 255), IM_COL32(110, 210, 90, 255), IM_COL32(80, 140, 240, 255)};
    const char* ids[3] = {"##x", "##y", "##z"};
    for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) {
        if (i > 0) ImGui::SameLine(0.0f, ImGui::GetStyle().ItemInnerSpacing.x);
        ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(each);
        const ImVec2 at = ImGui::GetCursorScreenPos();
        changed |= ImGui::DragFloat(ids[i], &value[i], speed, 0.0f, 0.0f, format);
        activated |= ImGui::IsItemActivated();
        deactivated |= ImGui::IsItemDeactivatedAfterEdit();
        ImGui::GetWindowDrawList()->AddRectFilled(at, ImVec2(at.x + 3.0f, at.y + ImGui::GetFrameHeight()), colors[i], 3.0f, ImDrawFlags_RoundCornersLeft);
    }
    ImGui::PopID();
    return changed;
}

struct Preset {
    const char* name;
    std::function<void(Material&)> apply;
};

const std::vector<Preset>& presets() {
    static const std::vector<Preset> list = {
        {"Plastic", [](Material& m) { m.metallic = 0.0f; m.roughness = 0.35f; m.reflectance = 0.5f; m.clearcoat = 0.0f; }},
        {"Rubber", [](Material& m) { m.metallic = 0.0f; m.roughness = 0.9f; m.reflectance = 0.35f; }},
        {"Steel", [](Material& m) { m.baseColor = glm::vec3(0.56f, 0.57f, 0.58f); m.metallic = 1.0f; m.roughness = 0.3f; }},
        {"Gold", [](Material& m) { m.baseColor = glm::vec3(1.0f, 0.766f, 0.336f); m.metallic = 1.0f; m.roughness = 0.2f; }},
        {"Copper", [](Material& m) { m.baseColor = glm::vec3(0.955f, 0.638f, 0.538f); m.metallic = 1.0f; m.roughness = 0.25f; }},
        {"Brushed", [](Material& m) { m.baseColor = glm::vec3(0.91f, 0.92f, 0.92f); m.metallic = 1.0f; m.roughness = 0.35f; m.anisotropy = 0.8f; }},
        {"Car paint", [](Material& m) { m.metallic = 0.4f; m.roughness = 0.4f; m.clearcoat = 1.0f; m.clearcoatRoughness = 0.03f; }},
        {"Velvet", [](Material& m) { m.metallic = 0.0f; m.roughness = 0.8f; m.sheenColor = glm::vec3(1.0f); m.sheenRoughness = 0.35f; }},
        {"Skin", [](Material& m) { m.baseColor = glm::vec3(0.85f, 0.62f, 0.52f); m.metallic = 0.0f; m.roughness = 0.55f; m.subsurface = 0.6f; m.subsurfaceColor = glm::vec3(0.9f, 0.3f, 0.2f); }},
        {"Glass", [](Material& m) { m.metallic = 0.0f; m.roughness = 0.02f; m.transmission = 1.0f; m.ior = 1.5f; m.opacity = 0.25f; }},
        {"Emissive", [](Material& m) { m.emissive = m.baseColor; m.emissiveIntensity = 8.0f; }},
    };
    return list;
}

}  // namespace

void Inspector::beginTransformEdit(EditorContext& ctx) {
    if (m_editing) return;
    m_editing = true;
    m_editStart.clear();
    for (EntityHandle e : ctx.topLevelSelection()) m_editStart.push_back({e, ctx.world.getLocalMatrix(e)});
}

void Inspector::endTransformEdit(EditorContext& ctx, const char* label) {
    if (!m_editing) return;
    m_editing = false;
    ctx.history->beginGroup(label);
    for (const Snapshot& s : m_editStart) {
        if (ctx.world.isAlive(s.entity)) ctx.history->commitLocalMatrix(s.entity, s.local, ctx.world.getLocalMatrix(s.entity));
    }
    ctx.history->endGroup();
    m_editStart.clear();
}

void Inspector::drawHeader(EditorContext& ctx, EntityHandle e) {
    World& world = ctx.world;
    if (m_bufferEntity != e || !ImGui::IsAnyItemActive()) {
        m_nameBuffer = world.getName(e);
        m_tagBuffer = world.getTag(e);
        m_bufferEntity = e;
    }
    if (m_focusName) {
        ImGui::SetKeyboardFocusHere();
        m_focusName = false;
    }
    ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail().x - 110.0f);
    if (ImGui::InputTextWithHint("##name", displayName(world, e).c_str(), &m_nameBuffer, ImGuiInputTextFlags_EnterReturnsTrue | ImGuiInputTextFlags_AutoSelectAll)) ctx.history->setName(e, m_nameBuffer);
    if (ImGui::IsItemDeactivatedAfterEdit()) ctx.history->setName(e, m_nameBuffer);
    ImGui::SameLine();
    ImGui::TextDisabled("#%u", e.index);
    ImGui::SameLine(ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail().x + ImGui::GetCursorPosX() - 36.0f);
    if (ImGui::SmallButton(ctx.pinInspector ? "Float" : "Pin")) {
        ctx.pinInspector = !ctx.pinInspector;
        m_dock = ctx.pinInspector;
    }
    if (ImGui::IsItemHovered()) ImGui::SetTooltip(ctx.pinInspector ? "Follow the selection again (Ctrl+Shift+I)" : "Dock the card to the right edge (Ctrl+Shift+I)");

    bool visible = world.isVisible(e);
    if (ImGui::Checkbox("Visible", &visible)) {
        ctx.history->beginGroup("Visibility");
        for (EntityHandle s : ctx.topLevelSelection()) ctx.history->setVisible(s, visible);
        ctx.history->endGroup();
    }
    const EntityHandle parent = world.getParent(e);
    if (!parent.isNull()) {
        ImGui::SameLine();
        ImGui::TextDisabled("in");
        ImGui::SameLine();
        if (ImGui::SmallButton(displayName(world, parent).c_str())) ctx.selection.set(parent);
        ImGui::SameLine();
        if (ImGui::SmallButton("Unparent")) ctx.history->setParent(e, NULL_ENTITY);
    }
    if (!ImGui::TreeNode("Tag, layers, occlusion")) return;
    bool occluder = (world.getRenderFlags(e) & RENDER_NO_OCCLUDER) == 0;
    if (ImGui::Checkbox("Occluder", &occluder)) {
        std::vector<EntityHandle> targets = ctx.topLevelSelection();
        auto set = [&world, targets](bool on) {
            for (EntityHandle t : targets) world.setRenderFlags(t, RENDER_NO_OCCLUDER, !on);
        };
        set(occluder);
        ctx.history->record("Occluder", [set, occluder] { set(occluder); }, [set, occluder] { set(!occluder); });
    }
    if (ImGui::IsItemHovered()) ImGui::SetTooltip("Off: drawn after the occlusion buffer so it never hides other objects");
    ImGui::SameLine();
    ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail().x);
    if (ImGui::InputTextWithHint("##tag", "tag", &m_tagBuffer, ImGuiInputTextFlags_EnterReturnsTrue)) ctx.history->setTag(e, m_tagBuffer);
    if (ImGui::IsItemDeactivatedAfterEdit()) ctx.history->setTag(e, m_tagBuffer);

    const uint32_t layer = world.getLayer(e);
    ImGui::AlignTextToFramePadding();
    ImGui::TextDisabled("Layers");
    for (int bit = 0; bit < 8; ++bit) {
        ImGui::SameLine(0.0f, 3.0f);
        const bool on = (layer & (1u << bit)) != 0;
        ImGui::PushID(bit);
        ImGui::PushStyleColor(ImGuiCol_Button, on ? ImVec4(1.0f, 0.69f, 0.26f, 0.55f) : ImGui::GetStyle().Colors[ImGuiCol_FrameBg]);
        if (ImGui::SmallButton(std::format("{}", bit).c_str())) {
            const uint32_t next = layer ^ (1u << bit);
            std::vector<EntityHandle> targets = ctx.topLevelSelection();
            std::vector<uint32_t> before;
            for (EntityHandle t : targets) before.push_back(world.getLayer(t));
            for (EntityHandle t : targets) world.setLayer(t, next);
            ctx.history->record("Layer", [&world, targets, next] { for (EntityHandle t : targets) world.setLayer(t, next); }, [&world, targets, before] {
                for (size_t i = 0; i < targets.size(); ++i) world.setLayer(targets[i], before[i]);
            });
        }
        ImGui::PopStyleColor();
        ImGui::PopID();
    }
    ImGui::TreePop();
}

void Inspector::drawTransform(EditorContext& ctx, EntityHandle e) {
    if (!ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Transform", ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_DefaultOpen)) return;
    World& world = ctx.world;
    glm::vec3 position = world.getPosition(e);
    glm::vec3 scale = world.getScale(e);
    const glm::quat rotationQ = world.getRotation(e);
    glm::vec3 euler = quatToEuler(rotationQ);
    bool activated = false, deactivated = false;
    const glm::vec3 oldPosition = position;
    if (axisDrag("Position", position, 0.05f, "%.3f", activated, deactivated)) {
        const glm::vec3 delta = position - oldPosition;
        for (EntityHandle s : ctx.topLevelSelection()) world.setPosition(s, s == e ? position : world.getPosition(s) + delta);
    }
    if (activated) beginTransformEdit(ctx);
    if (deactivated) endTransformEdit(ctx, "Move");

    const glm::vec3 oldEuler = euler;
    if (axisDrag("Rotation", euler, 0.5f, "%.1f°", activated, deactivated)) {
        const glm::quat next = eulerToQuat(euler);
        const glm::quat delta = next * glm::inverse(eulerToQuat(oldEuler));
        for (EntityHandle s : ctx.topLevelSelection()) world.setRotation(s, s == e ? next : glm::normalize(delta * world.getRotation(s)));
    }
    if (activated) beginTransformEdit(ctx);
    if (deactivated) endTransformEdit(ctx, "Rotate");

    const glm::vec3 oldScale = scale;
    if (axisDrag("Scale", scale, 0.01f, "%.3f", activated, deactivated)) {
        glm::vec3 ratio(1.0f);
        for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) ratio[i] = std::abs(oldScale[i]) > 1e-6f ? scale[i] / oldScale[i] : 1.0f;
        for (EntityHandle s : ctx.topLevelSelection()) world.setScale(s, s == e ? scale : world.getScale(s) * ratio);
    }
    if (activated) beginTransformEdit(ctx);
    if (deactivated) endTransformEdit(ctx, "Scale");

    const glm::vec3 worldPosition = world.getWorldPosition(e);
    ImGui::TextDisabled("World  %.2f  %.2f  %.2f", worldPosition.x, worldPosition.y, worldPosition.z);
    ImGui::SameLine(ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail().x - 110.0f + ImGui::GetCursorPosX() - ImGui::GetCursorPosX());
    if (ImGui::SmallButton("Reset")) {
        ctx.history->beginGroup("Reset transform");
        for (EntityHandle s : ctx.topLevelSelection()) ctx.history->setLocalMatrix(s, compose(glm::vec3(0.0f), glm::quat(1.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f), glm::vec3(1.0f)));
        ctx.history->endGroup();
    }
    ImGui::SameLine();
    if (ImGui::SmallButton("Copy")) ImGui::SetClipboardText(std::format("{} {} {}", position.x, position.y, position.z).c_str());
}

void Inspector::drawMaterial(EditorContext& ctx, uint32_t id) {
    Engine& engine = ctx.engine;
    Material m = engine.material(id);
    static Material before;
    bool changed = false;
    bool activated = false;
    bool committed = false;
    auto track = [&] {
        activated |= ImGui::IsItemActivated();
        committed |= ImGui::IsItemDeactivatedAfterEdit();
    };
    ImGui::PushID(static_cast<int>(id));
    ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(-1.0f);
    changed |= ImGui::InputText("##materialName", &m.name);
    track();

    ImGui::TextDisabled("Presets");
    float x = ImGui::GetCursorPosX();
    const float right = ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail().x + x;
    for (const Preset& preset : presets()) {
        const float w = ImGui::CalcTextSize(preset.name).x + ImGui::GetStyle().FramePadding.x * 2.0f;
        if (x + w > right) {
            x = ImGui::GetStyle().WindowPadding.x;
        } else if (&preset != &presets().front()) {
            ImGui::SameLine(0.0f, 4.0f);
        }
        if (ImGui::SmallButton(preset.name)) {
            const Material old = m;
            preset.apply(m);
            engine.setMaterial(id, m);
            const Material now = m;
            ctx.history->record("Material preset", [&engine, id, now] { engine.setMaterial(id, now); }, [&engine, id, old] { engine.setMaterial(id, old); });
        }
        x = ImGui::GetItemRectMax().x - ImGui::GetWindowPos().x + 4.0f;
    }

    ImGui::SeparatorText("Surface");
    changed |= ImGui::ColorEdit3("Base color", &m.baseColor.x, ImGuiColorEditFlags_Float);
    track();
    changed |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Metallic", &m.metallic, 0.0f, 1.0f);
    track();
    changed |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Roughness", &m.roughness, 0.0f, 1.0f);
    track();
    changed |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Reflectance", &m.reflectance, 0.0f, 1.0f);
    track();
    changed |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Opacity", &m.opacity, 0.0f, 1.0f);
    track();
    if (ImGui::TreeNode("Emission")) {
        changed |= ImGui::ColorEdit3("Color", &m.emissive.x, ImGuiColorEditFlags_Float);
        track();
        changed |= ImGui::DragFloat("Intensity", &m.emissiveIntensity, 0.05f, 0.0f, 10000.0f, "%.2f");
        track();
        ImGui::TreePop();
    }
    if (ImGui::TreeNode("Clear coat")) {
        changed |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Amount", &m.clearcoat, 0.0f, 1.0f);
        track();
        changed |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Roughness##cc", &m.clearcoatRoughness, 0.0f, 1.0f);
        track();
        ImGui::TreePop();
    }
    if (ImGui::TreeNode("Sheen")) {
        changed |= ImGui::ColorEdit3("Color##sheen", &m.sheenColor.x, ImGuiColorEditFlags_Float);
        track();
        changed |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Roughness##sheen", &m.sheenRoughness, 0.0f, 1.0f);
        track();
        ImGui::TreePop();
    }
    if (ImGui::TreeNode("Anisotropy")) {
        changed |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Strength", &m.anisotropy, -1.0f, 1.0f);
        track();
        changed |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Rotation", &m.anisotropyRotation, 0.0f, 1.0f);
        track();
        ImGui::TreePop();
    }
    if (ImGui::TreeNode("Subsurface")) {
        changed |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Amount##ss", &m.subsurface, 0.0f, 1.0f);
        track();
        changed |= ImGui::ColorEdit3("Scatter", &m.subsurfaceColor.x, ImGuiColorEditFlags_Float);
        track();
        changed |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Thickness", &m.thickness, 0.0f, 1.0f);
        track();
        ImGui::TreePop();
    }
    if (ImGui::TreeNode("Transmission")) {
        changed |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Amount##tr", &m.transmission, 0.0f, 1.0f);
        track();
        changed |= ImGui::SliderFloat("IOR", &m.ior, 1.0f, 2.5f);
        track();
        ImGui::TreePop();
    }
    if (ImGui::TreeNode("Mapping")) {
        changed |= ImGui::DragFloat("UV scale", &m.uvScale, 0.01f, 0.01f, 1000.0f);
        track();
        changed |= ImGui::Checkbox("Triplanar", &m.triplanar);
        track();
        changed |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Normal strength", &m.normalStrength, 0.0f, 2.0f);
        track();
        changed |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Occlusion", &m.occlusion, 0.0f, 1.0f);
        track();
        changed |= ImGui::DragFloat4("Custom 0", &m.custom0.x, 0.01f);
        track();
        changed |= ImGui::DragFloat4("Custom 1", &m.custom1.x, 0.01f);
        track();
        ImGui::TreePop();
    }
    if (activated) before = engine.material(id);
    if (changed) engine.setMaterial(id, m);
    if (committed) {
        const Material old = before;
        const Material now = engine.material(id);
        ctx.history->record("Edit material", [&engine, id, now] { engine.setMaterial(id, now); }, [&engine, id, old] { engine.setMaterial(id, old); });
    }
    ImGui::PopID();
}

void Inspector::drawRender(EditorContext& ctx, EntityHandle e) {
    World& world = ctx.world;
    Engine& engine = ctx.engine;
    if (!ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Render", ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_DefaultOpen)) return;
    const RenderableComponent r = world.getRenderable(e);
    const uint32_t mesh = world.getMesh(e);
    auto assign = [&](uint32_t newMesh, uint32_t material, uint32_t shader, float alpha, const char* label, bool meshChanged, bool materialChanged, bool shaderChanged, bool alphaChanged) {
        ctx.history->beginGroup(label);
        for (EntityHandle s : ctx.topLevelSelection()) {
            const RenderableComponent cur = world.getRenderable(s);
            ctx.history->setRender(s, meshChanged ? newMesh : world.getMesh(s), materialChanged ? material : cur.material_uuid, shaderChanged ? shader : cur.shaderId, alphaChanged ? alpha : cur.alpha);
        }
        ctx.history->endGroup();
    };

    ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(-60.0f);
    if (ImGui::BeginCombo("Mesh", meshLabel(engine, mesh).c_str())) {
        if (ImGui::Selectable("empty", mesh == NO_MESH)) assign(NO_MESH, 0, 0, 1.0f, "Set mesh", true, false, false, false);
        for (const std::string& name : engine.availableMeshes()) {
            if (ImGui::Selectable(name.c_str(), meshLabel(engine, mesh) == name)) {
                const uint32_t id = engine.loadMesh(name);
                assign(id, 0, 0, 1.0f, "Set mesh", true, false, false, false);
            }
        }
        ImGui::EndCombo();
    }

    const uint32_t material = r.material_uuid;
    ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(-60.0f);
    if (ImGui::BeginCombo("Material", std::format("{}  #{}", engine.material(material).name, material).c_str(), ImGuiComboFlags_HeightLarge)) {
        ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(-1.0f);
        if (ImGui::IsWindowAppearing()) ImGui::SetKeyboardFocusHere();
        ImGui::InputTextWithHint("##mf", "filter", &m_materialFilter);
        if (ImGui::Selectable("+ New material")) {
            Material fresh = engine.material(material);
            fresh.name = std::format("Material {}", engine.materialCount());
            const uint32_t id = engine.createMaterial(fresh);
            if (id != 0) assign(0, id, 0, 1.0f, "New material", false, true, false, false);
        }
        for (uint32_t id = 0; id < engine.materialCount(); ++id) {
            const std::string label = std::format("{}  #{}", engine.material(id).name, id);
            if (!m_materialFilter.empty() && fuzzyScore(m_materialFilter, label) <= 0.0f) continue;
            if (ImGui::Selectable(label.c_str(), id == material)) assign(0, id, 0, 1.0f, "Assign material", false, true, false, false);
        }
        ImGui::EndCombo();
    }
    const std::vector<std::string> shaders = engine.shaderNames();
    ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(-60.0f);
    if (ImGui::BeginCombo("Shader", r.shaderId < shaders.size() ? shaders[r.shaderId].c_str() : std::format("shader {}", r.shaderId).c_str())) {
        for (uint32_t id = 0; id < shaders.size(); ++id) {
            if (ImGui::Selectable(shaders[id].c_str(), id == r.shaderId)) assign(0, 0, id, 1.0f, "Set shader", false, false, true, false);
        }
        ImGui::EndCombo();
    }
    static float alphaBefore = 1.0f;
    float alpha = r.alpha;
    ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(-60.0f);
    if (ImGui::SliderFloat("Alpha", &alpha, 0.0f, 1.0f)) {
        for (EntityHandle s : ctx.topLevelSelection()) world.setAlpha(s, alpha);
    }
    if (ImGui::IsItemActivated()) alphaBefore = r.alpha;
    if (ImGui::IsItemDeactivatedAfterEdit()) {
        for (EntityHandle s : ctx.topLevelSelection()) world.setAlpha(s, alphaBefore);
        assign(0, 0, 0, alpha, "Alpha", false, false, false, true);
    }
    if (ImGui::TreeNodeEx("Material##editor", 0, "Edit material  ·  %s", engine.material(material).name.c_str())) {
        if (material == 0) ImGui::TextDisabled("Default material: shared by every object without one");
        drawMaterial(ctx, material);
        ImGui::TreePop();
    }
}

void Inspector::drawComponents(EditorContext& ctx, EntityHandle e, bool expand) {
    World& world = ctx.world;
    const EntityDescription description = world.describeEntity(e);
    if (!ImGui::CollapsingHeader(std::format("Components ({})###components{}", description.components.size(), expand ? "Open" : "").c_str(), expand ? ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_DefaultOpen : 0)) return;
    for (const auto& [name, text] : description.components) {
        ImGui::PushID(name.c_str());
        const bool open = ImGui::TreeNodeEx("##component", ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_DefaultOpen | ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_AllowOverlap, "%s", name.c_str());
        ImGui::SameLine(ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail().x + ImGui::GetCursorPosX() - 22.0f);
        if (ImGui::SmallButton("x")) {
            const std::string before = text;
            const std::string component = name;
            world.removeComponentByName(e, component);
            ctx.history->record("Remove component", [&world, e, component] { world.removeComponentByName(e, component); }, [&world, e, component, before] { world.setComponentFromText(e, component, before); });
            if (open) ImGui::TreePop();
            ImGui::PopID();
            break;
        }
        if (open) {
            if (name == "Light") {
                if (LightComponent* light = world.get<LightComponent>(e)) {
                    static LightComponent before;
                    if (!ImGui::IsAnyItemActive()) before = *light;
                    bool committed = false;
                    auto track = [&] { committed |= ImGui::IsItemDeactivatedAfterEdit(); };
                    int type = static_cast<int>(light->type);
                    if (ImGui::Combo("Type", &type, "Directional\0Point\0Spot\0")) {
                        light->type = static_cast<LightComponent::Type>(type);
                        committed = true;
                    }
                    ImGui::ColorEdit3("Color", &light->color.x, ImGuiColorEditFlags_Float);
                    track();
                    ImGui::DragFloat("Intensity", &light->intensity, 0.05f, 0.0f, 100000.0f);
                    track();
                    if (light->type != LightComponent::Type::Directional) {
                        ImGui::DragFloat("Range", &light->range, 0.1f, 0.01f, 100000.0f);
                        track();
                    }
                    if (light->type == LightComponent::Type::Spot) {
                        ImGui::SliderFloat("Inner cone", &light->innerConeDegrees, 0.0f, 89.0f);
                        track();
                        ImGui::SliderFloat("Outer cone", &light->outerConeDegrees, 0.0f, 89.0f);
                        track();
                    }
                    ImGui::SliderFloat("Specular", &light->specular, 0.0f, 1.0f);
                    track();
                    if (committed) {
                        const LightComponent old = before;
                        const LightComponent now = *light;
                        before = now;
                        ctx.history->record("Edit light", [&world, e, now] { if (auto* l = world.get<LightComponent>(e)) *l = now; }, [&world, e, old] { if (auto* l = world.get<LightComponent>(e)) *l = old; });
                    }
                }
            } else if (name == "Camera") {
                if (CameraComponent* camera = world.get<CameraComponent>(e)) {
                    ImGui::SliderFloat("FOV", &camera->fov, 10.0f, 150.0f);
                    ImGui::DragFloat("Near", &camera->nearPlane, 0.01f, 0.001f, 100.0f);
                    ImGui::DragFloat("Far", &camera->farPlane, 1.0f, 1.0f, 1000000.0f);
                    const bool active = world.getActiveCamera() == e;
                    if (ImGui::Button(active ? "Active camera" : "Make active")) world.setActiveCamera(active ? NULL_ENTITY : e);
                }
            } else {
                const std::string key = name + "#" + std::to_string(e.index);
                if (m_componentEditing != key) m_componentBuffer = text;
                ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(-1.0f);
                const bool enter = ImGui::InputText("##text", &m_componentBuffer, ImGuiInputTextFlags_EnterReturnsTrue);
                if (ImGui::IsItemActive()) m_componentEditing = key;
                if (enter || ImGui::IsItemDeactivatedAfterEdit()) {
                    if (!ctx.history->setComponentText(e, name, m_componentBuffer)) ctx.toast(std::format("'{}' rejected that value", name), ToastKind::Error);
                    m_componentEditing.clear();
                }
                if (ImGui::IsItemHovered()) ImGui::SetTooltip("Serialized text; Enter applies (undoable)");
            }
            ImGui::TreePop();
        }
        ImGui::PopID();
    }
    for (const auto& [name, text] : description.scripts) ImGui::BulletText("script  %s", name.c_str());

    ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(-1.0f);
    if (ImGui::BeginCombo("##add", "+ Add component")) {
        for (const std::string& name : world.componentNames()) {
            const bool present = std::any_of(description.components.begin(), description.components.end(), [&](const auto& c) { return c.first == name; });
            if (present) continue;
            if (ImGui::Selectable(name.c_str())) {
                if (world.addComponentByName(e, name)) {
                    const std::string component = name;
                    const std::string after = world.componentText(e, component).value_or("");
                    ctx.history->record("Add component", [&world, e, component, after] { world.setComponentFromText(e, component, after); }, [&world, e, component] { world.removeComponentByName(e, component); });
                }
            }
        }
        ImGui::EndCombo();
    }
}

glm::vec2 Inspector::placeCard(EditorContext& ctx, EntityHandle e, float width, float height) {
    const ImGuiViewport* viewport = ImGui::GetMainViewport();
    float left = viewport->WorkPos.x + 8.0f;
    float right = viewport->WorkPos.x + viewport->WorkSize.x - 8.0f;
    const float mid = viewport->WorkPos.x + viewport->WorkSize.x * 0.5f;
    for (const glm::vec4& panel : ctx.panels) {
        if (panel.x + panel.z * 0.5f < mid) {
            left = std::max(left, panel.x + panel.z + 8.0f);
        } else {
            right = std::min(right, panel.x - 8.0f);
        }
    }
    const float top = viewport->WorkPos.y + 8.0f;
    const float bottom = viewport->WorkPos.y + viewport->WorkSize.y - 40.0f;
    const float statsTop = ctx.rightColumnTop;
    auto clampY = [&](float x, float y) {
        const float minY = x + width > viewport->WorkPos.x + viewport->WorkSize.x - 330.0f ? statsTop : top;
        return std::clamp(y, minY, std::max(minY, bottom - height));
    };
    const glm::vec2 docked(right - width, clampY(right - width, top));
    const glm::vec4 bounds = ctx.world.getWorldBounds(e);
    const glm::vec3 anchor = bounds.w > 0.0f ? glm::vec3(bounds) : ctx.world.getWorldPosition(e);
    const std::optional<glm::vec2> screen = ctx.engine.worldToScreen(anchor);
    m_leader.reset();
    if (!screen || screen->x < left - 200.0f || screen->x > right + 200.0f || screen->y < top - 200.0f || screen->y > bottom + 200.0f) return docked;
    float radius = 24.0f;
    if (bounds.w > 0.0f) {
        const Camera& camera = ctx.world.camera();
        const glm::vec3 up = glm::normalize(glm::cross(glm::cross(cameraForward(camera), glm::vec3(0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f)), cameraForward(camera)));
        if (const std::optional<glm::vec2> edge = ctx.engine.worldToScreen(anchor + up * bounds.w)) radius = std::max(radius, glm::length(*edge - *screen));
    }
    const float gap = 28.0f;
    const glm::vec4 self(screen->x - radius - gap, screen->y - radius - gap, screen->x + radius + gap, screen->y + radius + gap);
    const glm::vec2 size = ctx.engine.windowSize();
    std::vector<glm::vec4> nearby;
    const Camera& camera = ctx.world.camera();
    const float depth = std::max(glm::dot(anchor - camera.getPosition(), cameraForward(camera)), 0.1f);
    const glm::vec3 cameraUp = glm::normalize(glm::cross(glm::cross(cameraForward(camera), glm::vec3(0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f)), cameraForward(camera)));
    const float worldPerPixel = (camera.isOrthographic() ? camera.getOrthoHeight() : 2.0f * depth * std::tan(glm::radians(camera.getFov()) * 0.5f)) / std::max(size.y, 1.0f);
    const std::vector<EntityHandle> around = ctx.world.overlapSphere(anchor, (width + radius + gap * 2.0f) * worldPerPixel);
    for (size_t i = 0; i < around.size() && nearby.size() < 64; ++i) {
        const EntityHandle other = around[i];
        if (other == e || !ctx.world.isVisible(other)) continue;
        const glm::vec4 b = ctx.world.getWorldBounds(other);
        const glm::vec3 c = b.w > 0.0f ? glm::vec3(b) : ctx.world.getWorldPosition(other);
        const std::optional<glm::vec2> s = ctx.engine.worldToScreen(c);
        if (!s) continue;
        const std::optional<glm::vec2> edge = b.w > 0.0f ? ctx.engine.worldToScreen(c + cameraUp * b.w) : std::nullopt;
        const float r = std::max(edge ? glm::length(*edge - *s) : 0.0f, 16.0f);
        if (r > size.y * 0.3f) continue;
        nearby.emplace_back(s->x - r, s->y - r, s->x + r, s->y + r);
    }
    auto overlap = [](const glm::vec4& a, const glm::vec4& b) {
        return std::max(0.0f, std::min(a.z, b.z) - std::max(a.x, b.x)) * std::max(0.0f, std::min(a.w, b.w) - std::max(a.y, b.y));
    };
    struct Candidate {
        glm::vec2 at;
        int side;
        bool dock;
    };
    std::vector<Candidate> candidates;
    for (float fy : {0.3f, 0.0f, 0.7f, 1.0f}) {
        candidates.push_back({{self.z, clampY(self.z, screen->y - height * fy)}, 1, false});
        candidates.push_back({{self.x - width, clampY(self.x - width, screen->y - height * fy)}, -1, false});
    }
    const float cx = std::clamp(screen->x - width * 0.5f, left, std::max(left, right - width));
    candidates.push_back({{cx, self.w}, 0, false});
    candidates.push_back({{cx, self.y - height}, 0, false});
    candidates.push_back({docked, 2, true});
    candidates.push_back({{left, clampY(left, top)}, -2, true});
    float bestScore = std::numeric_limits<float>::max();
    Candidate best = candidates[candidates.size() - 2];
    for (const Candidate& candidate : candidates) {
        const glm::vec4 rect(candidate.at.x, candidate.at.y, candidate.at.x + width, candidate.at.y + height);
        if (rect.x < left - 1.0f || rect.z > right + 1.0f || rect.y < top - 1.0f || rect.w > bottom + 1.0f) continue;
        float covered = overlap(rect, self) * 50.0f;
        for (const glm::vec4& other : nearby) covered += overlap(rect, other);
        const float area = width * height;
        float score = covered / area * 1000.0f + (candidate.dock ? 120.0f : glm::distance(candidate.at + glm::vec2(width, height) * 0.5f, *screen) * 0.05f);
        if (glm::distance(candidate.at, m_lastTarget) < 40.0f) score -= 25.0f;
        if (score < bestScore) {
            bestScore = score;
            best = candidate;
        }
    }
    if (best.side == 1 || best.side == -1) m_side = best.side;
    m_lastTarget = best.at;
    if (best.dock) m_leader = *screen;
    return best.at;
}

void Inspector::draw(EditorContext& ctx) {
    m_sections.clear();
    if (!ctx.showInspector || ctx.zen) return;
    const EntityHandle e = ctx.primary();
    if (!ctx.world.isAlive(e)) return;

    const ImGuiViewport* viewport = ImGui::GetMainViewport();
    const float width = 340.0f;
    const float maxHeight = ctx.pinInspector ? viewport->WorkSize.y - 60.0f : std::min(560.0f, std::max(240.0f, viewport->WorkSize.y * 0.55f));
    ImGuiWindowFlags flags = ImGuiWindowFlags_NoCollapse | ImGuiWindowFlags_NoFocusOnAppearing | ImGuiWindowFlags_AlwaysAutoResize;
    if (!ctx.pinInspector) {
        const glm::vec2 target = placeCard(ctx, e, width, std::min(m_lastHeight, maxHeight));
        if (m_cardPos.x < 0.0f) m_cardPos = target;
        const float k = 1.0f - std::exp(-ImGui::GetIO().DeltaTime * 14.0f);
        m_cardPos += (target - m_cardPos) * k;
        ImGui::SetNextWindowPos(ImVec2(m_cardPos.x, m_cardPos.y), ImGuiCond_Always);
        m_setPos = m_cardPos;
    } else if (m_dock) {
        float right = viewport->WorkPos.x + viewport->WorkSize.x - 12.0f;
        for (const glm::vec4& panel : ctx.panels) {
            if (panel.x + panel.z * 0.5f > viewport->WorkPos.x + viewport->WorkSize.x * 0.5f) right = std::min(right, panel.x - 10.0f);
        }
        ImGui::SetNextWindowPos(ImVec2(right - width, ctx.rightColumnTop), ImGuiCond_Always);
        m_dock = false;
    } else {
        ImGui::SetNextWindowPos(ImVec2(viewport->WorkPos.x + viewport->WorkSize.x - width - 12.0f, ctx.rightColumnTop), ImGuiCond_FirstUseEver);
    }
    ImGui::SetNextWindowSizeConstraints(ImVec2(width, 80.0f), ImVec2(width, maxHeight));
    ImGui::SetNextWindowBgAlpha(0.92f);
    const std::string title = ctx.selection.size() > 1 ? std::format("{} selected  ·  {}###Inspector", ctx.selection.size(), displayName(ctx.world, e)) : std::format("{}###Inspector", displayName(ctx.world, e));
    if (!ImGui::Begin(title.c_str(), &ctx.showInspector, flags)) {
        ImGui::End();
        return;
    }
    if (!ctx.pinInspector) {
        const ImVec2 pos = ImGui::GetWindowPos();
        if ((std::abs(pos.x - m_setPos.x) > 2.0f || std::abs(pos.y - m_setPos.y) > 2.0f) && ImGui::IsMouseDragging(ImGuiMouseButton_Left) && ImGui::IsWindowFocused()) {
            ctx.pinInspector = true;
            ctx.toast("Card pinned. Ctrl+Shift+I to let it follow the selection again");
        }
    }
    m_lastHeight = ImGui::GetWindowHeight();
    if (m_leader && !ctx.pinInspector) {
        const ImVec2 pos = ImGui::GetWindowPos();
        const ImVec2 size = ImGui::GetWindowSize();
        const ImVec2 from(std::clamp(m_leader->x, pos.x, pos.x + size.x), std::clamp(m_leader->y, pos.y, pos.y + size.y));
        ImDrawList* draw = ImGui::GetBackgroundDrawList();
        draw->AddLine(from, ImVec2(m_leader->x, m_leader->y), IM_COL32(255, 184, 72, 150), 1.5f);
        draw->AddCircleFilled(ImVec2(m_leader->x, m_leader->y), 3.5f, IM_COL32(255, 184, 72, 200));
    }
    World& world = ctx.world;
    const bool light = world.get<LightComponent>(e) != nullptr;
    const bool camera = world.get<CameraComponent>(e) != nullptr;
    const bool gadget = (light || camera) && world.getMesh(e) == NO_MESH;
    ImGui::PushItemWidth(-90.0f);
    drawHeader(ctx, e);
    m_sections = "header";
    drawTransform(ctx, e);
    m_sections += ",transform";
    if (!gadget) {
        drawRender(ctx, e);
        m_sections += ",render";
    }
    drawComponents(ctx, e, gadget);
    m_sections += ",components";
    ImGui::PopItemWidth();
    ImGui::End();
}

```

Editor/Inspector/Inspector.cppm
```
module;

#include <optional>

#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

export module Editor.inspector;

import Editor.context;
import Engine.Render.entity;
import Engine.glm;

export class Inspector {
  public:
    void draw(EditorContext& ctx);
    void focusName() { m_focusName = true; }
    void dock() { m_dock = true; }
    [[nodiscard]] const std::string& sections() const { return m_sections; }

  private:
    struct Snapshot {
        EntityHandle entity;
        glm::mat4 local{1.0f};
    };

    void drawHeader(EditorContext& ctx, EntityHandle e);
    void drawTransform(EditorContext& ctx, EntityHandle e);
    void drawRender(EditorContext& ctx, EntityHandle e);
    void drawMaterial(EditorContext& ctx, uint32_t id);
    void drawComponents(EditorContext& ctx, EntityHandle e, bool expand);
    glm::vec2 placeCard(EditorContext& ctx, EntityHandle e, float width, float height);
    void beginTransformEdit(EditorContext& ctx);
    void endTransformEdit(EditorContext& ctx, const char* label);

    bool m_focusName = false;
    std::string m_nameBuffer;
    std::string m_tagBuffer;
    EntityHandle m_bufferEntity;
    std::vector<Snapshot> m_editStart;
    bool m_editing = false;
    glm::vec2 m_cardPos{-1.0f};
    glm::vec2 m_setPos{-1.0f};
    float m_lastHeight = 300.0f;
    std::string m_componentBuffer;
    std::string m_componentEditing;
    std::string m_materialFilter;
    bool m_materialDirty = false;
    bool m_dock = false;
    int m_side = 1;
    glm::vec2 m_lastTarget{-1.0f};
    std::optional<glm::vec2> m_leader;
    std::string m_sections;
};

```

Editor/Gizmo/Gizmo.cpp
```
module;

#include <imgui.h>
#include <ImGuizmo.h>

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstring>
#include <format>
#include <optional>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

module Editor.gizmo;

import Editor.context;
import Engine.engine;
import Engine.World;
import Engine.History;
import Engine.GizmoMath;
import Engine.camera;
import Engine.Render.entity;
import Engine.glm;

namespace {

const glm::vec4 kAxisColors[3] = {glm::vec4(1.0f, 0.32f, 0.32f, 1.0f), glm::vec4(0.45f, 0.95f, 0.35f, 1.0f), glm::vec4(0.35f, 0.60f, 1.0f, 1.0f)};
const char* kAxisNames[3] = {"X", "Y", "Z"};

glm::mat4 translation(const glm::vec3& t) { return glm::translate(glm::mat4(1.0f), t); }

std::optional<glm::vec3> planeHit(const Ray& ray, const glm::vec3& point, const glm::vec3& normal) { return gizmo::rayPlane(ray, point, normal); }

float snapValue(float value, float step) { return step > 0.0f ? std::round(value / step) * step : value; }

}  // namespace

void TransformTool::capture(EditorContext& ctx) {
    m_starts.clear();
    glm::vec3 sum(0.0f);
    for (EntityHandle e : ctx.topLevelSelection()) {
        const EntityHandle parent = ctx.world.getParent(e);
        m_starts.push_back({e, ctx.world.getLocalMatrix(e), ctx.world.getWorldMatrix(e), parent.isNull() ? glm::mat4(1.0f) : ctx.world.getWorldMatrix(parent)});
        sum += glm::vec3(m_starts.back().world[3]);
    }
    m_pivot = m_starts.empty() ? glm::vec3(0.0f) : sum / static_cast<float>(m_starts.size());
}

void TransformTool::begin(EditorContext& ctx, ModalKind kind) {
    if (ctx.selection.empty()) {
        ctx.toast("Nothing selected", ToastKind::Warning);
        return;
    }
    if (m_kind != ModalKind::None) {
        for (const Start& s : m_starts) ctx.world.setLocalMatrix(s.entity, s.local);
    } else {
        capture(ctx);
        m_axis = -1;
        m_plane = false;
        m_localAxis = false;
    }
    if (m_starts.empty()) return;
    m_kind = kind;
    m_typed.clear();
    m_startMouse = ctx.mouse;
    m_accumulatedAngle = 0.0f;
    const std::optional<glm::vec2> screen = ctx.engine.worldToScreen(m_pivot);
    m_pivotScreen = screen && std::isfinite(screen->x) && std::isfinite(screen->y) ? *screen : ctx.mouse;
    m_lastAngleVector = glm::normalize(ctx.mouse - m_pivotScreen + glm::vec2(1e-3f, 0.0f));
    const Ray ray = ctx.engine.screenRay(ctx.mouse.x, ctx.mouse.y);
    const glm::vec3 viewNormal = cameraForward(ctx.world.camera());
    m_startHit = planeHit(ray, m_pivot, viewNormal).value_or(m_pivot);
    m_startParameter = 0.0f;
    ctx.modalActive = true;
}

glm::vec3 TransformTool::axisDirection(EditorContext& ctx) const {
    if (m_axis < 0) return cameraForward(ctx.world.camera());
    glm::vec3 axis(0.0f);
    axis[m_axis] = 1.0f;
    if (m_localAxis && !m_starts.empty()) axis = glm::normalize(glm::vec3(m_starts.back().world[m_axis]));
    return axis;
}

std::string TransformTool::status() const {
    const char* verb = m_kind == ModalKind::Move ? "Move" : m_kind == ModalKind::Rotate ? "Rotate" : "Scale";
    std::string axis = m_axis < 0 ? (m_kind == ModalKind::Move ? "view plane" : m_kind == ModalKind::Rotate ? "view axis" : "uniform") : std::string(m_plane ? "plane " : "") + kAxisNames[m_axis] + (m_localAxis ? " local" : "");
    std::string value;
    if (!m_typed.empty()) {
        value = "= " + m_typed + "|";
    } else if (m_kind == ModalKind::Move) {
        value = std::format("{:.3f}, {:.3f}, {:.3f}", m_delta.x, m_delta.y, m_delta.z);
    } else if (m_kind == ModalKind::Rotate) {
        value = std::format("{:.1f}°", m_value);
    } else {
        value = std::format("×{:.3f}", m_value);
    }
    return std::format("{}  {}  {}", verb, axis, value);
}

void TransformTool::apply(EditorContext& ctx) {
    const ImGuiIO& io = ImGui::GetIO();
    const bool snap = ctx.snapping != io.KeyCtrl;
    const std::optional<double> typed = m_typed.empty() ? std::nullopt : evaluateExpression(m_typed);
    const Ray ray = ctx.engine.screenRay(ctx.mouse.x, ctx.mouse.y);
    const glm::vec3 axis = axisDirection(ctx);

    if (m_kind == ModalKind::Move) {
        glm::vec3 delta(0.0f);
        if (typed) {
            glm::vec3 dir = m_axis < 0 ? glm::vec3(1.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f) : axis;
            delta = dir * static_cast<float>(*typed);
        } else if (m_axis >= 0 && !m_plane) {
            const float t = gizmo::closestParameterOnAxis(ray, m_pivot, axis);
            const Ray startRay = ctx.engine.screenRay(m_startMouse.x, m_startMouse.y);
            const float t0 = gizmo::closestParameterOnAxis(startRay, m_pivot, axis);
            float d = t - t0;
            if (snap) d = snapValue(d, ctx.snapMove);
            delta = axis * d;
        } else {
            const glm::vec3 normal = m_axis >= 0 ? axis : cameraForward(ctx.world.camera());
            const Ray startRay = ctx.engine.screenRay(m_startMouse.x, m_startMouse.y);
            const std::optional<glm::vec3> a = planeHit(startRay, m_pivot, normal);
            const std::optional<glm::vec3> b = planeHit(ray, m_pivot, normal);
            if (a && b) delta = *b - *a;
            if (snap) delta = glm::vec3(snapValue(delta.x, ctx.snapMove), snapValue(delta.y, ctx.snapMove), snapValue(delta.z, ctx.snapMove));
        }
        m_delta = delta;
        for (const Start& s : m_starts) {
            const glm::mat4 world = translation(delta) * s.world;
            ctx.world.setLocalMatrix(s.entity, glm::inverse(s.parentWorld) * world);
        }
        return;
    }

    if (m_kind == ModalKind::Rotate) {
        const glm::vec2 current = ctx.mouse - m_pivotScreen;
        if (glm::length(current) > 2.0f) {
            const glm::vec2 v = glm::normalize(current);
            const float step = std::atan2(m_lastAngleVector.x * v.y - m_lastAngleVector.y * v.x, glm::dot(m_lastAngleVector, v));
            if (std::isfinite(step)) m_accumulatedAngle += step;
            m_lastAngleVector = v;
        }
        const glm::vec3 viewDir = cameraForward(ctx.world.camera());
        const float facing = glm::dot(axis, viewDir) >= 0.0f ? 1.0f : -1.0f;
        float degrees = typed ? static_cast<float>(*typed) : glm::degrees(m_accumulatedAngle) * facing;
        if (snap && !typed) degrees = snapValue(degrees, ctx.snapAngle);
        m_value = degrees;
        const glm::mat4 rotation = glm::translate(glm::mat4(1.0f), m_pivot) * glm::mat4_cast(glm::angleAxis(glm::radians(typed ? degrees : -degrees), axis)) * glm::translate(glm::mat4(1.0f), -m_pivot);
        for (const Start& s : m_starts) ctx.world.setLocalMatrix(s.entity, glm::inverse(s.parentWorld) * rotation * s.world);
        return;
    }

    const float startDistance = std::max(glm::length(m_startMouse - m_pivotScreen), 4.0f);
    float factor = typed ? static_cast<float>(*typed) : glm::length(ctx.mouse - m_pivotScreen) / startDistance;
    if (snap && !typed) factor = std::max(snapValue(factor, ctx.snapScale), ctx.snapScale);
    m_value = factor;
    for (const Start& s : m_starts) {
        glm::vec3 offset = glm::vec3(s.world[3]) - m_pivot;
        glm::mat4 world = s.world;
        if (m_axis < 0) {
            offset *= factor;
            world[0] *= factor;
            world[1] *= factor;
            world[2] *= factor;
        } else {
            const glm::vec3 a = axis;
            offset += a * (glm::dot(offset, a) * (factor - 1.0f));
            for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {
                const glm::vec3 column(world[c]);
                const float along = glm::length(column) > 1e-6f ? std::abs(glm::dot(glm::normalize(column), a)) : 0.0f;
                world[c] = glm::vec4(column * (1.0f + (factor - 1.0f) * along), 0.0f);
            }
        }
        world[3] = glm::vec4(m_pivot + offset, 1.0f);
        ctx.world.setLocalMatrix(s.entity, glm::inverse(s.parentWorld) * world);
    }
}

void TransformTool::commit(EditorContext& ctx, const char* label) {
    ctx.history->beginGroup(label);
    for (const Start& s : m_starts) {
        if (!ctx.world.isAlive(s.entity)) continue;
        ctx.history->commitLocalMatrix(s.entity, s.local, ctx.world.getLocalMatrix(s.entity));
    }
    ctx.history->endGroup();
}

void TransformTool::cancel(EditorContext& ctx) {
    for (const Start& s : m_starts) {
        if (ctx.world.isAlive(s.entity)) ctx.world.setLocalMatrix(s.entity, s.local);
    }
}

void TransformTool::update(EditorContext& ctx) {
    if (m_kind == ModalKind::None) return;
    const ImGuiIO& io = ImGui::GetIO();
    auto axisKey = [&](ImGuiKey key, int axis) {
        if (!ImGui::IsKeyPressed(key, false)) return;
        if (m_axis == axis && m_plane == io.KeyShift) {
            if (m_localAxis) {
                m_axis = -1;
                m_localAxis = false;
            } else {
                m_localAxis = true;
            }
        } else {
            m_axis = axis;
            m_plane = io.KeyShift;
            m_localAxis = ctx.localSpace;
        }
    };
    axisKey(ImGuiKey_X, 0);
    axisKey(ImGuiKey_Y, 1);
    axisKey(ImGuiKey_Z, 2);
    if (ImGui::IsKeyPressed(ImGuiKey_G, false)) begin(ctx, ModalKind::Move);
    if (ImGui::IsKeyPressed(ImGuiKey_R, false)) begin(ctx, ModalKind::Rotate);
    if (ImGui::IsKeyPressed(ImGuiKey_S, false)) begin(ctx, ModalKind::Scale);

    for (ImWchar c : io.InputQueueCharacters) {
        if ((c >= '0' && c <= '9') || c == '.' || c == '-' || c == '+' || c == '*' || c == '/' || c == '(' || c == ')' || c == '^' || c == 'p' || c == 'i') m_typed.push_back(static_cast<char>(c));
    }
    if (ImGui::IsKeyPressed(ImGuiKey_Backspace)) {
        if (!m_typed.empty()) m_typed.pop_back();
    }

    apply(ctx);

    const bool confirm = ImGui::IsKeyPressed(ImGuiKey_Enter, false) || ImGui::IsKeyPressed(ImGuiKey_KeypadEnter, false) || ImGui::IsMouseClicked(ImGuiMouseButton_Left);
    const bool abort = ImGui::IsKeyPressed(ImGuiKey_Escape, false) || ImGui::IsMouseClicked(ImGuiMouseButton_Right);
    const char* label = m_kind == ModalKind::Move ? "Move" : m_kind == ModalKind::Rotate ? "Rotate" : "Scale";
    ctx.modeLabel = label;
    ctx.hint("X/Y/Z", "axis (again: local)");
    ctx.hint("Shift+X/Y/Z", "plane");
    ctx.hint("0-9 . -", "type value");
    ctx.hint("Ctrl", ctx.snapping ? "free" : "snap");
    ctx.hint("Enter/Click", "apply");
    ctx.hint("Esc/RMB", "cancel");

    const glm::vec3 axis = axisDirection(ctx);
    if (m_axis >= 0) {
        const glm::vec4 color = kAxisColors[m_axis];
        if (m_plane) {
            for (int other = 0; other < 3; ++other) {
                if (other == m_axis) continue;
                glm::vec3 dir(0.0f);
                dir[other] = 1.0f;
                ctx.engine.debugLine(m_pivot - dir * 2000.0f, m_pivot + dir * 2000.0f, kAxisColors[other], true);
            }
        } else {
            ctx.engine.debugLine(m_pivot - axis * 2000.0f, m_pivot + axis * 2000.0f, color, true);
        }
    }
    ImDrawList* draw = ImGui::GetForegroundDrawList();
    if (m_kind != ModalKind::Move) draw->AddLine(ImVec2(m_pivotScreen.x, m_pivotScreen.y), ImVec2(ctx.mouse.x, ctx.mouse.y), IM_COL32(255, 190, 90, 160), 1.5f);
    const std::string text = status();
    const ImVec2 at(ctx.mouse.x + 18.0f, ctx.mouse.y + 18.0f);
    const ImVec2 textSize = ImGui::CalcTextSize(text.c_str());
    draw->AddRectFilled(ImVec2(at.x - 8.0f, at.y - 5.0f), ImVec2(at.x + textSize.x + 8.0f, at.y + textSize.y + 5.0f), IM_COL32(18, 20, 24, 225), 6.0f);
    draw->AddText(at, IM_COL32(255, 200, 110, 255), text.c_str());

    if (confirm) {
        commit(ctx, label);
        m_kind = ModalKind::None;
        ctx.modalActive = false;
    } else if (abort) {
        cancel(ctx);
        m_kind = ModalKind::None;
        ctx.modalActive = false;
    }
}

void TransformTool::drawHandles(EditorContext& ctx) {
    const EntityHandle primary = ctx.primary();
    if (ctx.handles == HandleMode::Off || m_kind != ModalKind::None || !ctx.world.isAlive(primary) || ctx.flying) {
        m_handleDragging = false;
        return;
    }
    const glm::vec2 size = ctx.engine.windowSize();
    const Camera& camera = ctx.world.camera();
    ImGuizmo::SetOrthographic(camera.isOrthographic());
    ImGuizmo::SetDrawlist(ImGui::GetBackgroundDrawList());
    ImGuizmo::SetRect(0.0f, 0.0f, size.x, size.y);
    ImGuizmo::AllowAxisFlip(false);
    const glm::mat4 view = camera.getViewMatrix();
    const float aspect = size.x / std::max(size.y, 1.0f);
    const float halfHeight = camera.getOrthoHeight() * 0.5f;
    const glm::mat4 projection = camera.isOrthographic() ? glm::ortho(-halfHeight * aspect, halfHeight * aspect, -halfHeight, halfHeight, camera.getNearPlane(), camera.getFarPlane()) : glm::perspective(glm::radians(camera.getFov()), aspect, camera.getNearPlane(), camera.getFarPlane());
    glm::mat4 matrix = ctx.world.getWorldMatrix(primary);
    const ImGuizmo::OPERATION op = ctx.handles == HandleMode::Translate ? ImGuizmo::TRANSLATE : ctx.handles == HandleMode::Rotate ? ImGuizmo::ROTATE : ImGuizmo::SCALE;
    const ImGuizmo::MODE mode = ctx.localSpace || ctx.handles == HandleMode::Scale ? ImGuizmo::LOCAL : ImGuizmo::WORLD;
    const bool snap = ctx.snapping != ImGui::GetIO().KeyCtrl;
    const float step = ctx.handles == HandleMode::Translate ? ctx.snapMove : ctx.handles == HandleMode::Rotate ? ctx.snapAngle : ctx.snapScale;
    const float snapValues[3] = {step, step, step};
    const bool changed = ImGuizmo::Manipulate(&view[0][0], &projection[0][0], op, mode, &matrix[0][0], nullptr, snap ? snapValues : nullptr);
    const bool using_ = ImGuizmo::IsUsing();
    if (using_ && !m_handleDragging) {
        m_handleDragging = true;
        capture(ctx);
        m_handleStarts = m_starts;
        m_handlePrimaryStart = ctx.world.getWorldMatrix(primary);
    }
    if (m_handleDragging && changed) {
        const glm::mat4 delta = matrix * glm::inverse(m_handlePrimaryStart);
        for (const Start& s : m_handleStarts) {
            const glm::mat4 world = s.entity == primary ? matrix : delta * s.world;
            ctx.world.setLocalMatrix(s.entity, glm::inverse(s.parentWorld) * world);
        }
    }
    if (!using_ && m_handleDragging) {
        m_handleDragging = false;
        m_starts = m_handleStarts;
        commit(ctx, ctx.handles == HandleMode::Translate ? "Move" : ctx.handles == HandleMode::Rotate ? "Rotate" : "Scale");
    }
}

void TransformTool::nudge(EditorContext& ctx, const glm::vec3& screenDirection) {
    const auto top = ctx.topLevelSelection();
    if (top.empty()) return;
    const Camera& camera = ctx.world.camera();
    const glm::vec3 forward = cameraForward(camera);
    const glm::vec3 right = glm::normalize(glm::cross(forward, glm::vec3(0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f)));
    auto dominant = [](glm::vec3 v) {
        v.y = 0.0f;
        if (std::abs(v.x) >= std::abs(v.z)) return glm::vec3(v.x >= 0.0f ? 1.0f : -1.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f);
        return glm::vec3(0.0f, 0.0f, v.z >= 0.0f ? 1.0f : -1.0f);
    };
    const glm::vec3 dir = dominant(right) * screenDirection.x + dominant(forward) * screenDirection.z + glm::vec3(0.0f, screenDirection.y, 0.0f);
    const float step = ctx.snapMove * (ImGui::GetIO().KeyShift ? 10.0f : 1.0f);
    ctx.history->beginGroup("Nudge");
    for (EntityHandle e : top) {
        const EntityHandle parent = ctx.world.getParent(e);
        const glm::mat4 parentWorld = parent.isNull() ? glm::mat4(1.0f) : ctx.world.getWorldMatrix(parent);
        const glm::mat4 world = translation(dir * step) * ctx.world.getWorldMatrix(e);
        ctx.history->setLocalMatrix(e, glm::inverse(parentWorld) * world);
    }
    ctx.history->endGroup();
}

void TransformTool::resetTransform(EditorContext& ctx, int part) {
    const auto top = ctx.topLevelSelection();
    if (top.empty()) return;
    ctx.history->beginGroup(part == 0 ? "Reset position" : part == 1 ? "Reset rotation" : "Reset scale");
    for (EntityHandle e : top) {
        const glm::vec3 position = ctx.world.getPosition(e);
        const glm::quat rotation = ctx.world.getRotation(e);
        const glm::vec3 scale = ctx.world.getScale(e);
        const glm::vec3 p = part == 0 ? glm::vec3(0.0f) : position;
        const glm::quat r = part == 1 ? glm::quat(1.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f) : rotation;
        const glm::vec3 s = part == 2 ? glm::vec3(1.0f) : scale;
        ctx.history->setLocalMatrix(e, glm::translate(glm::mat4(1.0f), p) * glm::mat4_cast(r) * glm::scale(glm::mat4(1.0f), s));
    }
    ctx.history->endGroup();
}

```

Editor/Gizmo/Gizmo.cppm
```
module;

#include <string>
#include <vector>

export module Editor.gizmo;

import Editor.context;
import Engine.Render.entity;
import Engine.glm;

export enum class ModalKind { None, Move, Rotate, Scale };

export class TransformTool {
  public:
    void begin(EditorContext& ctx, ModalKind kind);
    void update(EditorContext& ctx);
    void drawHandles(EditorContext& ctx);
    [[nodiscard]] bool active() const { return m_kind != ModalKind::None; }
    [[nodiscard]] bool handleDragging() const { return m_handleDragging; }
    void nudge(EditorContext& ctx, const glm::vec3& screenDirection);
    void resetTransform(EditorContext& ctx, int part);

  private:
    struct Start {
        EntityHandle entity;
        glm::mat4 local{1.0f};
        glm::mat4 world{1.0f};
        glm::mat4 parentWorld{1.0f};
    };

    void capture(EditorContext& ctx);
    void apply(EditorContext& ctx);
    void commit(EditorContext& ctx, const char* label);
    void cancel(EditorContext& ctx);
    [[nodiscard]] glm::vec3 axisDirection(EditorContext& ctx) const;
    [[nodiscard]] std::string status() const;

    ModalKind m_kind = ModalKind::None;
    std::vector<Start> m_starts;
    glm::vec3 m_pivot{0.0f};
    glm::vec2 m_startMouse{0.0f};
    glm::vec2 m_pivotScreen{0.0f};
    glm::vec3 m_startHit{0.0f};
    float m_startParameter = 0.0f;
    int m_axis = -1;
    bool m_plane = false;
    bool m_localAxis = false;
    std::string m_typed;
    float m_value = 0.0f;
    glm::vec3 m_delta{0.0f};
    float m_accumulatedAngle = 0.0f;
    glm::vec2 m_lastAngleVector{1.0f, 0.0f};
    bool m_handleDragging = false;
    std::vector<Start> m_handleStarts;
    glm::mat4 m_handlePrimaryStart{1.0f};
};

```
